  `test_plan_contract_v1350` and `test_receipt_seams_v13520` (StopIteration)
  — and both now poll the listing until the session's row is there (15 s).
  With the fix: 508 passed under a 0.5 s hold and a 2 s hold, both bytecode
  settings; 78 more files that read `waiting_on`, asks or the mission view
  passed under both holds (`waiting_on` reads the in-memory registry, which
  holds the ask before the publish). A NEW test that reads the listing after
  seeing an ask polls it.

- **Shell lines from a PROJECT's or PLUGIN's command file always ask — in
  every posture, past every grant; the user's own command files follow the
  posture** (v1.381.0; the user's decision 2026-10-23; a doer + a reviewer
  that found 9 more defects, each red first and mutation-checked).
  `harness/command_shell.py`: a turn-scoped ContextVar rule (`set_turn_rule` /
  `reset_turn_rule`, armed LAST and reset FIRST in `start_build_turn` beside
  the pin, hold and branch tokens) + `setup.command_shell` (SETUP_KEY; each
  project/plugin command's own shell lines, ≤ 16 commands × 20 lines × 400
  chars, written ONLY by `start_build_turn` BEFORE any generator opens —
  a failed write refuses the command with 503 `COMMAND_RECORD_SENTENCE`, no
  model call; whitelisted in `_clean_setup`, ignored by PATCH, a chat PUT
  409s, copied by Branch from here, kept by Rewind — it can only ask more; a
  repeat of one command replaces its older copy; a command with only an empty
  ```! block is kept when it names its turn). `tool_check` (git guard FIRST,
  then `command_check`) is reached from `spend_check` on `shell` (the
  registered `SandboxedShellTool`), `process_start`, `process_write` →
  `SpendCheck(once_only=True, reason=…)` with `once_reason: "command"`. THE
  COMMAND'S OWN TURN and its daemon continuations (Resume when ready /
  Continue on another account — `continued_turn` finds the newest
  non-continuation question, SKIPPING steer notes, which carry the
  carry-on's turn id) card EVERY command-running call; a LATER user turn
  cards only a call whose text contains a recorded line (`normalize`:
  case-folded, quotes stripped, spaces collapsed, `\` / `^` / backtick
  continuations joined; word boundaries; syntax-only and comment lines never
  match) and names the matched line (masked, ≤ 80 chars). Rewordings,
  PowerShell vs bash, script files and input split across process writes are
  NOT caught in a later turn (stated in the module docstring and the model's
  note). Helpers inherit (context copy + `HelperParent.command_rule` +
  `pinned()`); the runtime passes `once_only`/`once_reason` to
  `approvals.request`, so a helper's card is Allow-once only (this also fixed
  the git guard's helper card, which offered "Allow for this run" and read it
  as once); `core/events._drop_build_turn_scope` clears the rule in handlers'
  context; the POST lane refuses through the once-only path; Local only keeps
  its own card AND now carries `once_reason`. The folder layer's
  `plugins.toml` checks (`chat_turn.harness_check_runner`) now ask the
  carrier tool's spend check first and are NOT run on their own when it would
  ask (the model is told to run them; that call asks) — the git guard had the
  same hole (a check that commits/pushes on a session branch no longer runs by
  itself). Personal = `~/.claude/commands` (`CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` honoured; a
  session folder AT home lists it once, as personal). The model's note says
  the user is asked before each shell command in this turn and, in a later
  turn, before any command containing one of these lines. KEYBOARD:
  `lib/dockAsk.enterAllows` — an approval with `onceOnly` and a SAFETY reason
  (`command`, `git`, `page`) is NEVER allowed by Enter (DockAsk in chat and the
  harness, PaneAsk); Esc declines; the keys line reads "Click Allow once to run
  it · Esc to decline"; ordinary, local-only and plan-door cards still answer
  Enter (the doer's own live run approved a card with a second Enter right
  after sending). `lib/onceReason.ts` is the reason whitelist. OPEN: a
  project command that asks for commands in PROSE (no `!` syntax) gets no
  card; shell lines inside a folder's/plugin's SKILL are not covered; a typed
  "continue" after a restart-cut command turn and the act turn after an
  approved plan are later turns (exact lines only); a short recorded line like
  `git status` asks on every later run in that session; worst-case record
  ~140 KB of setup. Pins: `tests/test_build_command_shell_{ask,review}_v1381.py`,
  `dashboard/__tests__/ij-harness-command-shell{,-review}-v1381.test.tsx`.

- **Claude Code's `/` in the IJ harness: a typed `/name words` RUNS it;
  Claude Code's own command files are read, never executed; a Build session
  needs NO folder** (v1.379.0; the user: "I want to be able to use the same
  / commands i do here there in the IJ harness" and "it should not require
  that I am in a specific folder"; a doer + a reviewer that found 16 more
  defects, each red first and mutation-checked). WHY IT FAILED: the harness
  read all of `GET /skills`, but `lib/slash.slashTokenAt` ends the token at
  the first space, so `/pixio-skill draw a cat` + Enter went as plain text.
  TYPED: a message STARTING with `/<known name>` invokes it (`BuildTurnBody`
  `skill`/`folder_skill` + the words; a bare skill = "Follow the X skill.";
  a custom command = `command` + words); an unknown `/word` (`/etc/hosts`)
  is text; Enter on a half-typed name picks the highlighted row, an EXACT
  name leads (`/review` once ran `review-pr`); Tab puts "/name "; a typed
  command queued while a reply runs waits in the queue (never a note).
  COMMANDS (`harness/claude_commands.py`, `GET /build/sessions/{id}/commands`
  — name/description/argument-hint/source, NEVER a body): user
  (`claude_dir()/commands`, honouring `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` — and
  `skills.framework.claude_dir()` now does too, unchanged when unset),
  project (`.claude/commands` — a worktree session reads the worktree's
  copy, then the picked folder's, like folder rules), installed+enabled
  plugins and the claude.ai `plugins/synced/<org>_<account>` folder of the
  SIGNED-IN account only (`signed_in_bucket` from `.claude.json`
  `oauthAccount`; the `CLAUDE_CODE_*_UUID` env vars are IGNORED — in a daemon
  started from a Claude Code session they are the parent's; no account →
  every folder); marketplace catalogs never; Claude Code's own `.trash`
  folders (`plugins/.trash`, `skills/.trash`) skipped by the SKILL registry
  too (chat as well: the two cowork skills now resolve to the installed copy;
  same 43 names). A command whose name a skill also has is NOT listed —
  typing it runs the skill (Claude Code's rule). EXPANSION (Claude Code's
  way, read from its docs AND binary): `$ARGUMENTS`, `$N` ZERO-based (`$0` =
  first word; docs verbatim), `$ARGUMENTS[N]` (a missing one stays as
  written), named arguments (missing → ""), `\$`, "ARGUMENTS: …" appended when
  nothing was filled; the user's words are BRACKETED while filling, so a
  typed `@secret.txt` or `` !`del` `` never attaches a file or adds the shell
  sentence — only the command's own text names files (`@$0` still reads the
  file the words name); `@path` (≤ 32 looked up) goes through
  `sessions.folder_files`, which now REFUSES network spellings (`\\host\…`,
  `//host/…`, `\\?\UNC\…`) and device paths other than `\\?\C:\` BEFORE
  touching the disk — `fs_policy._canonical` on a UNC path opened an SMB
  connection (a sign-in hash leak to that host from a repository's command
  file) — shares allowed only when the session's own folder is on one;
  `!`…`` / ```` ```! ```` lines are NEVER run by the app (spied: no
  `core/procs` / `subprocess` call) — one sentence asks the model to run them
  with its shell tool, under the session's posture; `allowed-tools` grants
  nothing (proven through the real `invoke`), `model` is ignored (the hold);
  project + plugin command text is promptguard-scanned BEFORE the words are
  filled (a flagged one reaches the model as `[BLOCKED…]` + one
  `context.blocked` naming the command), personal commands are the user's
  words. Arguments split by `_split_args`: a backslash is a letter except
  before a quote (POSIX `shlex` ate `C:\Users\x`) — a departure from Claude
  Code's bash parse (`a\ b`). The question shows what was typed; later turns'
  history reads the expansion (`commandText`). BUILT-INS under Claude Code's
  names: `/compact [focus]` (`POST …/compact {focus}` → `compact.focus_line`;
  no words = byte-identical prompt), `/plan [words]`, `/rewind`, `/model
  [name]` (`modelByName`: the one available model by that name, the
  session's provider breaks a tie, else the menu), `/clear` (`/reset`,
  `/new`: a new session on the same folder), `/resume` (`/continue`),
  `/usage` (`/cost`, `/stats`), `/init` (folder sessions only, an ordinary
  message). The harness's own commands still win over a user's skill of the
  same name (app controls). NO FOLDER: `POST /build/sessions` takes no
  folder (`setup.no_folder`, `_clean_setup` keeps it); at the FIRST turn the
  session gets `<chat_files_root>/<YYYY-MM-DD> <first words>` (a command's
  first message: "<command> <words>") through `documents/workfolder.
  make_chat_workfolder` + `folder_title` (strips `<>:"/\|?*`, controls,
  trailing dots/spaces, ≤ 60; same name → "(2)"; a race → the next free
  name); a first message that never starts takes it back while empty; a
  folder the user deleted is made AGAIN at the same place (`_make_again`)
  unless its parent is gone (an unplugged drive is refused in words); archive
  / delete remove it only when empty (a hidden file or subfolder keeps it),
  inside the chat files folder (a junction pointing out is never followed)
  and no other session — archived ones count — uses it (`kept_folder`);
  Branch from here SHARES the origin's folder; git routes answer "This
  session has no folder of its own, so it is not in a repository and has no
  branch."; library reflection is skipped (a paid call on the plan); `@`
  lists that folder. New session in front of a folderless session opens
  folderless (never the Terminals folder). Left pane: one "No folder" group
  with its own +; below xl the pane scrolls (the New session form drew over
  the next session at 390 px). OPEN: a repository command's shell lines run
  WITHOUT a card under approve-for-me / yolo (the model runs them under the
  posture — a product decision for the user); managed-settings
  `enabledPlugins` (HKLM policy / Program Files drop-ins) not read; attaching
  a folder later; `/clear <name>` ignores the name; Rewind restores a
  command's own @files as chips; a parent Claude Code session's
  `CLAUDE_CODE_ORGANIZATION_UUID`/`_ACCOUNT_UUID` are not in
  `claude_markers` (a dev daemon passes them to panes); not driven in the
  packaged app. Pins: `tests/test_build_slash_commands_v1379.py`,
  `tests/test_build_no_folder_v1379.py`,
  `tests/test_build_slash_nofolder_review_v1379.py`,
  `dashboard/__tests__/ij-harness-{slash-commands,no-folder,
  slash-nofolder-review,new-folder-nofolder-review}-v1379.test.tsx`; four
  `ij-harness-slash-at-v1373` pins moved on purpose (new command rows).
  v1.379.0 WAS NOT PUBLISHED (its Release gate went red; v1.380.0 carries
  all of it). `test_build_turn_finished_v1374`'s long-turn test failed there
  and in three loaded local runs with "not enough values to unpack (expected
  1, got 0)", and the first fix (wait for the event) was only half of it:
  (1) the test moved its fake clock as soon as the turn LOG existed, but the
  log exists before `recorded_turn` runs its first line, which is where the
  turn stamps its start (`_ReplyWatch.started_at`) — on a loaded runner the
  clock moved first, the turn measured ~0 s and was RIGHTLY never announced
  (a 0.5 s delay at the top of `recorded_turn` reproduces it, even with the
  event wait); `_long_turn` now waits for the turn's first token in
  `lg.frames` (every frame there has passed through `recorded_turn`); (2) the
  notice is published AFTER the last frame and its sync handler runs on a
  worker, so the test `until`s the event and the handler's verdict (a 0.5 s
  delay before the notice's row read reproduces that half). ALSO:
  the v1343 "real files untouched" guards errored ONCE on the user's
  `~/.claude.json` (its `mcpServers` view changed at 19:02:49 mid-run); every
  test that reads Claude Code's settings (21 files, 672 tests) left the file
  untouched, and a second full run with a before/after snapshot was clean —
  Claude Code itself rewrote it. The guard names only the file; if it fires
  again, snapshot the view (hash per project) before believing a test.

- **The IJ harness is MEASURED again: a benchmark turn runs AS a Build
  session; the walkthrough's gaps are written down** (v1.378.0, IJ harness
  part 2 Phase 7; a doer + a reviewer that found 5 real defects, each red
  first and mutation-checked). BENCH: the Jarvis runner ran every task under
  the chat lane's "chat" session, so v1.377.0's Build env (the user's PATH)
  never reached a benchmark turn and nothing held the model; each task turn
  now runs under its own `build:bench-<hex>` session with `providers.hold`
  armed, `ask_user` off, and `session: "build"` in the Jarvis runner's
  identity (a new config folder — no older record is reused).
  `harness_bench/runners.session_ended_on_escape` ends that session on ANY
  way out (a Ctrl+C/Stop used to leave the turn's programs alive under a
  session nothing would end); the hold is armed inside it. The bench calls
  the chat lane directly, so library reflection, the finish notice, Build
  compaction and session-row writes (all in `routes/build.py`) never run for
  it. RESULTS (in `docs/IJ-HARNESS-PLAN-2.md` Phase 7, read from the bench's
  own `results.json`): Claude Code 30/30, Codex 30/30, Jarvis on Codex 30/30
  (gained `hard-expression-evaluator`), Jarvis on Claude 29/30 and glm
  28/30 — both lost `hard-expression-evaluator` in the full run and passed
  it TWICE alone (variance); no part 2 tool was called in 90 Jarvis runs;
  "is not recognized" fell to 0 on every setup. WALKTHROUGH
  (`docs/IJ-HARNESS-WALKTHROUGH.md`, 21 JPEGs in `docs/walkthrough/`,
  checked for e-mail/account/key/project content): every step possible;
  presses IJ 11/9/11 vs Claude Code 6–9/4–7/8–14 (Claude Code counted from
  its desktop page, estimated — the page does not say Manual is the default
  and says local dev servers need no approval) — "no more presses" is NOT
  met for tasks 1 and 2; gaps listed there. SCREEN FIXES: New session opens
  on the folder of the session in front (an archived one falls back to the
  old default); Changes opens on the Branch view only for a LIVE branch
  (`branch_changes` covers committed + uncommitted + untracked; a removed
  worktree or merged branch opens "This session"); after Commit the branch
  is read BEFORE the "Committed" note, newest read wins (a sequence guard);
  a Build reply line saves each step's target (`harness/step_target.py` is
  the Python twin of `lib/workTarget.stepTarget`, held equal by the 70-case
  fixture `dashboard/__tests__/fixtures/step-target-v1378.json` — change both
  together; the table found the TS 60-char cut splitting an emoji); a file
  named by its whole path is listed by its path inside the folder
  (`routes/chat_changes.py`, chat and Build). Pins:
  `tests/test_harness_bench_build_session{,_review}_v1378.py`,
  `tests/test_step_target_v1378.py`, `tests/test_chat_changes_whole_path_v1378.py`,
  `dashboard/__tests__/{ij-harness-new-folder,ij-harness-changes-branch-default,
  ij-harness-phase7-review}-v1378.test.tsx`, `step-target-twin-v1378.test.ts`.
  SAME RELEASE (test-only): the merged full suite failed three
  `test_iron_proxy_service_v1301` tests that start the Python stand-in
  ("Iron-Proxy is on but not answering", `owned` False) and passed alone; an
  11 s simulated start (`FAKE_IRON_PROXY_START_DELAY`) reproduces all three
  on the old shape. `_use_fake_bundle` now sets `START_TIMEOUT_S` = 60 for
  every stand-in start in that file (a hang detector, the v1.374.1 rule),
  the lease wait 90 s, the crash-orphan test's own timeout 240 s.

- **A Build session's commands see the user's PATH and home, never a
  secret; the rest of the app stays scrubbed** (v1.377.0, IJ harness part 2
  Phase 6 — the user's decision of 2026-10-23 after the Phase 4 review found
  `git`/`node`/`npm`/`python` unusable from the agent's shell; doer + a
  reviewer that found 7 real defects incl. two leaks, each red first and
  mutation-checked). THE SWITCH: `harness/command_env.is_build_call(
  session_id)` — ONLY a `build:<id>` call or a helper registered under a
  `build:` parent; `command_env(modify_env, session_id)` gives `shell` (the
  registered `SandboxedShellTool`, native runs) and `process_start`
  `sandbox/native.build_env()`; EVERYTHING else (chat, agents, schedules,
  goals, workflows, the phone, a chat's helper, a mission, `Build:`, "")
  gets `scrubbed_env()` byte for byte (pinned). Low trust still refuses a
  native shell; Local only still cards every command; terminal PANES are
  untouched. `build_env()`: the daemon's environment MINUS (by NAME) secret
  words (KEY, TOKEN, SECRET, PASSWORD/PASSWD/PASSPHRASE, PASS/PWD as a part —
  never `PWD` itself —, CREDENTIAL, SESSION, COOKIE, PRIVATE, ASKPASS,
  WEBHOOK, JWT, AUTH but never AUTHOR, PAT/DSN parts), vendor families
  (ANTHROPIC_, OPENAI_, GEMINI_, GOOGLE_, AZURE_, AWS_, GH_, GITHUB_, HF_,
  IRONJARVIS_, IRON_PROXY_, PLAYWRIGHT_, CLAUDE_CODE_, …), exact names
  (ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE, VIRTUAL_ENV, PyInstaller's), a parent Claude Code
  session's markers, git's repo redirects, and any value pointing into the
  app's own install/venv; and (review) by VALUE: whatever
  `detections.redact.mask` would change or a known credential shape drops
  the variable, EXCEPT a URL's `user:password@`, which is removed and the
  variable KEPT (a dropped `PIP_INDEX_URL` installs from the public index);
  folder lists (PATH-like) are never value-judged (`sk-learn-env`). KEPT:
  PATH, HOME/USERPROFILE/HOMEDRIVE/HOMEPATH/APPDATA/LOCALAPPDATA/TEMP, the
  user's git/npm/pip/nvm/pnpm settings, `SSH_AUTH_SOCK`, `GCM_*`,
  `KUBECONFIG`, cloud ACCOUNT SELECTORS (`AWS_PROFILE`, …) and key PATHS
  (`GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS`) — a dropped selector sends `aws`/
  `gcloud` to the user's DEFAULT (often production) account. `GIT_CONFIG_
  COUNT` + its `KEY_n`/`VALUE_n` pairs are judged as ONE unit (dropping
  `GIT_CONFIG_KEY_0` for "KEY" made EVERY git command refuse to start — found
  live). `GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0` added. The model is told the truth:
  `build_env_tools` (the tools really on that PATH; a Store python alias
  does not count) → `build_env_description` in the shell description and the
  runtime note (Build only). THE GIT GUARD (v1.375.0) now resolves the USER'S
  OWN ALIASES (global, XDG, repo config, includes, `GIT_CONFIG_COUNT`,
  alias-of-alias, `!` shell aliases) — `git pf` with `pf = push --force`
  force-pushed uncarded; also closed `git -c alias.push=status push -f`.
  PHASE 6 TOUCHES: Next step (`harness/followups.py`, the chat's
  `chat_followups` setting, off by default; the session's pair under hold +
  pin, billed, Local only respected; a press fills the box, never sends);
  Branch from here (`lib/buildNext.ts` + the build routes: a new session
  with the conversation up to the message; on a git session its OWN new
  branch from the newest commit on the original branch at or before the last
  line it holds, else where the original started — never the base's later
  tip, never later turns' commits; uncommitted work is NOT carried and
  `branched_from.code` + the header say so; model, reasoning, account,
  approval, Local only and Browser address copied; act mode, no plan, no
  grants); Search sessions; keys (`lib/harnessKeys.ts`: Ctrl+N new session —
  the desktop app's `buildMenu` binds no Ctrl+N; a browser tab keeps it, so
  the hint names it only in the desktop app —, Esc stops a running reply but
  never a real popup's Escape (`aria-haspopup`/combobox/dialog/menu/listbox
  — an open DISCLOSURE (`aria-expanded`) is not a popup; the review found Esc
  dead once any fold was open), IME, another field, Ctrl+L, Ctrl+K = the
  model menu inside the harness taken in CAPTURE so the palette never sees
  it, the palette everywhere else); Rewind / a question branch put back
  pictures, `@` files and the `/` skill (`skillSource` saved on questions);
  rewinding committed work says the commits stay; "Summarizing…" on return
  mid-Compact; ONE sign-in rule (signed-out PICKABLE in menu and chip — the
  daemon's rule; off/missing blocked); "Ask first" = "Asks before it edits
  files, uses the internet, runs commands, or clicks or types in a browser."
  (`lib/permissionLevels`); the chat gauge's unknown-window words
  (`lib/contextWords.ts`). SCREENSHOTS ARE NOT OCR-SCANNED (decided with
  measurements: cheap, but flagged ordinary AI-app admin and docs pages —
  false positives that would lower trust in exactly the harness's work —
  and missed faint text; `page_read` text is already fenced and scanned).
  FOUND, NOT FIXED HERE: Anthropic's safety classifier answers `stop_reason:
  "refusal"` (`stop_details.category`, e.g. "cyber") with no content, and
  Iron Jarvis turns it into an EMPTY reply + "Press Retry" — the classifier
  judges the whole conversation, so every later message in that session is
  refused (`claude_native/transport.assemble` treats it as handled since
  v1.300.0; `adapters/anthropic._finish` maps it to "stop"); FIXED in
  v1.378.0 (see that bullet). KNOWN: commands can print a sign-in the user's own tools hold (`gh
  auth token`, `git credential fill`); system-wide git aliases are not read;
  a restored `@` file deleted since is refused at send with a sentence.
  Pins: `tests/test_build_{env,env_review,phase6,phase6_review,git_aliases,
  branch_point}_v1377.py`, `dashboard/__tests__/{ij-harness-phase6,
  ij-harness-keys-review}-v1377.test.tsx`.

  on the loaded runner the polite close + kill + folder removal took longer,
  the fallback ("did not answer; terminating its job" — in the CI log) found
  the kill handle already taken by the close still in flight, and answered
  [] while that close went on to end every process. The wait is now

  bar. Pin: `test_a_slow_close_still_reports_the_processes_it_ended` (a

  the reason LAST — `KeyError: 'closed_reason'` on the loaded runner. It now
  waits for the reason itself; a 0.5 s folder removal holds the gap open
  (old wait: CI's exact KeyError on an idle machine). The waitFor rule, for
  Python: wait for the field you assert, never for a step that lands
  before it.

- **A Build session in a git folder works on ITS OWN BRANCH in a worktree
  BESIDE the repository; the user's main checkout is touched by Merge only;
  the agent's commit/push always asks and history is never rewritten**
  (v1.375.0, IJ harness part 2 Phase 4 — `docs/IJ-HARNESS-PLAN-2.md`; the
  user's decisions 1-3; a doer + a reviewer that found 12 real defects, some
  of them able to lose the user's work, each red first and mutation-checked).
  `harness/branches.py` (start, status, Branch diff vs the merge base,
  commit, push, PR via `gh`, merge, `remove_worktree`), `harness/git_guard.py`
  (the agent's git), `daemon/routes/build_git.py`, `HarnessBranch.tsx`,
  `lib/buildGit.ts`. START: `ij/<slug>-<4 hex>` in
  `<repo-parent>/<repo>` + `WORKTREES_SUFFIX` (`.ij-worktrees`); New
  session's "Work on a new branch" switch (on by default, git folders only);
  no repo / no git / no commits / bare / drive root / SUBMODULE (its worktree
  landed inside the superproject's `.git`) say why; a refused start UNDOES
  its branch (only while it still points where it was made) and worktree.
  FACTS: `setup.git` (repo, checkout = the picked folder, main, worktree,
  branch, base, base commit, name, later pushed/PR/merged/removed), its own
  whitelist in `_clean_setup` (every thread — a chat thread's forged record
  gets 404 from every git route and no reader trusts it), written ONLY by the
  /build routes, records land on the facts AS STORED (a stale copy dropped a
  PR link). The session's folder IS the worktree; it groups under the PICKED
  folder, takes that folder's project (the worktree is outside every
  project root; the prompt's project folder line names the worktree and a
  paragraph says the main checkout is the user's — helpers get it too), and
  reads the harness layer / rule files from the worktree's copy, else the
  main checkout's (untracked files are not in a new worktree); the library's
  edits go to the copy the session READS. EVERY git/gh child through
  `git/integration.run_git` → `core/procs` (bounded, tree-killed, off the
  loop, ONE git action per session at a time — a per-session lock taken off
  the loop; the e2e found a Merge re-read holding the folder during Remove),
  `git_env()` (GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0, and DROPS `GIT_DIR`/`GIT_INDEX_FILE`/…
  — git sets them for hooks and every Build call then acted on THAT repo),
  reads with `GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS=0` (a status never rewrites the user's
  index), output masked.
  COMMIT/MERGE/PR refuse until the worktree's HEAD is back on ITS branch (a
  `git switch main` in the worktree made Commit commit onto the user's
  main). Commit's message is drafted by the session's own model (hold +
  pin + Local only; the demo model drafts nothing). MERGE: a checked-out
  base merges THERE with `git merge --no-ff` after `merge-tree --write-tree`
  says no conflict and that checkout is clean (a hook stop → `merge
  --abort`); a base checked out nowhere gets the same merge commit via
  `commit-tree` + `update-ref <new> <old>` after a dry-run `git fetch .
  <merge>:<base>` (git's own guard: a checkout mid-REBASE reads as
  "detached" and moving the base broke the user's rebase). DISCARD /
  Remove / Delete: the folder is RENAMED beside itself first (refused with
  nothing moved while a file is held; ~5 s of retries), copied to
  `<home>/trash/<stamp>/` across drives (renamed back if the copy fails),
  then `git worktree remove <path>` (no `--force`) forgets ONLY this one —
  `git worktree prune` forgot every worktree whose folder was missing (an
  unplugged drive's). The branch always stays; Delete refuses in words if
  the folder cannot move. PR: `gh` found by `shutil.which`, `gh auth status`
  per host remembered 60 s, else the compare link; a title that would run
  through cmd.exe when `gh` is a `.cmd`/`.bat` is refused before the push;
  checks re-read every 60 s only while on screen; doctor row `check_gh`
  (developer rows). THE AGENT (`git_guard`, through `tools/base.SpendCheck`
  `once_only`/`reason` on `shell`, `process_start`, `process_write`):
  ONLY on a branch — REFUSED with no card in any posture: force push,
  deleting a remote branch, history rewrites (amend, rebase, pull
  --rebase, reset to another commit, filter-branch, update-ref), deleting /
  renaming / force-moving a branch or tag, `worktree remove/prune/move`,
  `gh repo delete`; CARDED Allow-once-only in every posture past every
  grant: commit, push, any `gh` change, git that throws work away (`reset
  --hard`, `clean -f`, `checkout -- .`, `restore`, `stash drop`) and `git
  config` writes; quotes stripped and `-c alias.x=` expanded (`cmd /c "git
  push"`, `bash -lc 'git commit'` slipped through); the POST lane refuses;
  proven on the REAL registry `invoke`, a helper run and the process tools.
  It reads command TEXT: a guardrail, not a sandbox (the user's own git
  aliases are not caught; `grep "git rebase"` reads as running it).
  THE AGENT'S SHELL CANNOT RUN GIT IN THE PRODUCT — not this phase's, but
  found by it: `sandbox.modify_env = "deny"` (the default) gives `shell` and
  the process tools `native.scrubbed_env()` — PATH = the app's own folder +
  System32 + Windows, no HOME/USERPROFILE — so `git`, `node`, `npm`,
  `python` do not resolve (only `py.exe`), and a full-path git cannot read
  the user's global identity or stored sign-in. The Branch buttons run in
  the app's own environment and work. Changing that is the user's decision.
  KNOWN: rewinding a committed turn puts its files back as uncommitted
  changes and does not say so; "dubious ownership" reads as "not a
  repository"; git < 2.38 cannot pre-check a merge; Local only shows the
  shell's own card before a refused git command; not driven against the
  real GitHub or in the packaged app. A branch status read runs ~13 git
  children (0.45 s idle): under a full local `-n auto` run one passed the
  shared test helper's old 30 s GET limit, now
  `test_build_sessions_v1367.GET_TIMEOUT_S` = 120 s (a hang detector, not a
  speed bar). Pins: `tests/test_build_git{,_agent,
  _pr,_review,_guard_review}_v1375.py`,
  `dashboard/__tests__/ij-harness-git-v1375.test.tsx`, e2e
  `dashboard/e2e/ij-harness-git.spec.ts`.

- **A Build session SHOWS its work: the plan under the reply, how full the
  conversation is, a session summary only Compact writes, and one notice for

  PLAN-2.md`; a doer + a reviewer that found 7 real defects, each red first

  decomposed helper's plan, re-read at turn end by `_with_helper_plans`)
  draw under the live reply through the chat's `PlanChecklist`

  "Plan · N of M done"; the reply line saves `plan` and `context`

  dashboard decoder). A Build plan CARRIES OVER: an unreached step reads
  "not started", the step it was on stays unticked (failed with a failed
  turn) — never the chat run's "Not attempted". METER
  (`components/harness/ContextMeter.tsx`, `lib/buildContext.ts`): numbers
  ONLY from the daemon's `routes/build.context_receipt` (the shared
  `chat_turn._apply_compaction`, so both chat lanes' `context` gain it too):
  it shows `percent` (RAW demand against the window — the planned figure
  can never pass 100%), offers Compact at the daemon's own `level` (≥
  `SUGGEST_AT`), and `window_known: false` means `window` is the app's
  `DEFAULT_WINDOW`, so the words say "of what Iron Jarvis sends this model
  at once … its own limit is not known" — never the model's limit;
  compaction off in Settings shows the number with no Compact. The right-hand
  group of the box WRAPS (measured at 390 px: Send was pushed ~60 px out).
  COMPACT (`harness/compact.py`, `POST /build/sessions/{id}/compact`,
  `/compact`): coverage ends on a turn boundary keeping
  `compaction.KEEP_RECENT` (6) lines; ONE `router.complete(pin=True)` on
  `_turn_pair`'s model under `set_hold`, the session's Iron-Proxy pin
  (`turn_account`) and `egress.local_only_scope`, bounded by
  `COMPACT_TIMEOUT_S` (240 → 504 in words); `compaction.verify` against the
  covered lines + `ledger_facts` limited to THE COVERED TURNS' OWN CALLS
  (`turn_windows`: each turn's first line to its last — a rewound turn's
  rows and a later turn's must never corroborate a path); the summarizer is
  asked for a third and the summary is KEPT ONLY WHEN LIGHTER than what it
  replaces; promptguard-scanned (`context.blocked`); billed to the session
  (`library._bill`); stored in `setup.compaction` by `sessions.set_compaction`,
  a locked write that re-checks the covered lines (`covered_key`) — a
  conversation that changed answers 409 `CHANGED`. Every parse, hash, ledger
  read and billing write is off the loop. A parked/signed-out account
  answers its own limit sentence (`limit_words`, 409); the demo model never
  writes one (before AND after the call). ONLY COMPACT WRITES THE SUMMARY:
  PATCH ignores a forged one, a Build session cannot be PUT, and a chat
  thread's forged record is never read (`_clean_setup` whitelists the key
  for EVERY thread so a setup write does not drop it; no chat lane reads it)
  — pinned. Later turns read
  `compact.history_for_turn` (the lines after the coverage) and the summary
  rides the harness block (`compact.set_summary` ContextVar, read in
  `HarnessTurn.block()` BEFORE the planner; not in the fingerprint);
  re-compaction feeds the old summary as `prior`; `sessions.cut_transcript`
  drops it when a rewind cuts any covered line (`survives_cut`), a refused
  rewind keeps it. A low-trust session may compact (no tools; the summarizer
  reads questions, replies and masked step inputs, never a tool's output) —
  decided and pinned. FINISH NOTICE: `build.turn_finished` (`EventType.
  BUILD_TURN_FINISHED`: session id, title, duration, outcome) is published by
  `recorded_turn` AFTER the reply line for a turn longer than
  `FINISH_NOTICE_AFTER_S` (60, the daemon's clock from the turn's start)
  that finished or failed — never for a Stop, a shutdown cut-off or an
  account-limit card, and never for a helper's own end. The bell pings once
  unless the user is LOOKING at that session (`lib/buildWatching`:
  `ij.build.looking` in localStorage, fresh 20 s, shared by every window)
  and a watched finish is not recounted after a reload (`ij.build.watched`);
  a quiet pop-out only records (`quietWindow()`); main.js
  `RUN_OUTCOME_AUDIT_TYPES` + `runOutcomeNotice` say it from the tray; the
  notifier's `bell_pings` keeps "This PC" quiet (one-notice fixture
  extended). KNOWN: Build turns still run the chat's own automatic
  compaction at 92% (transcript-checked only), so two summaries can stack;
  the CHAT page's gauge still calls an unknown window the model's; a return
  to a session mid-Compact shows no "Summarizing…" (a send is refused in
  words); turn windows rely on line stamps (a row on the same 15.6 ms tick
  at a turn's edge can still count). Pins: `tests/test_build_{compact,
  turn_finished,plan_lines}_v1374.py` (+ the review files),
  `dashboard/__tests__/{ij-harness-work,build-finish-notice}-v1374.test.tsx`.
  v1.374.1 (a REAL race + a load fix, both from v1.374.0's red Tests gate;
  Release was green and published): (1) `Orchestrator.cancel_session`'s
  CROSS-LOOP branch (a schedule fire runs on APScheduler's own loop) handed
  `task.cancel` over BEFORE it stamped the row, and `_finalize_cancelled`
  POPS `_stop_reasons` — when the run's loop finalized first, the stamp found
  no reason and saved "The agent was stopped before it finished." over
  Emergency stop's sentence. It now stamps + saves FIRST, then
  `call_soon_threadsafe(task.cancel)`, so the finalizer's write is always
  the last. Pin: `tests/test_emergency_stop_cross_loop_v13741.py` (the
  cancelling thread's `.get` held back 0.5 s — the old order gives CI's
  exact words; a plain cross-loop cancel keeps STOPPED_SUMMARY). (2) The two
  real-bundle Iron-Proxy tests ran against the app's 10 s
  `START_TIMEOUT_S`; a Node cold start (0.47 s idle here) passed it on the
  loaded runner. They set 60 s and print the whole status (its `error`) on
  failure. A TIMING SCRIPT run outside pytest must set its own
  `IRON_PROXY_DATA_DIR` (the conftest fixture is pytest-only): without it
  `IronProxyService.start()` ADOPTS the user's running proxy through
  `~/.iron-proxy/proxy.json` (read-only here, but never acceptable).

  SKILL.md is a 400 in words, NOT the 404) added to the harness block for
  that message (not the fingerprint). `@` lists the SESSION folder's files
  (`lib/atMenuFiles` with an opt-in `fuzzy`; the chat keeps exact matching,
  pinned); `BuildTurnBody.files` → `harness/sessions.folder_files` (OFF the
  loop: joined to the folder, RESOLVED — links, junctions, `..`, 8.3 names —,
  inside the resolved folder, a file, `fs_read_ok`; one bad entry refuses the
  whole message with a 400 sentence and nothing starts; blank entries are not
  files); the saved question gains `filePaths` + `skill`. THE READ LIMIT:
  the lane reads `chat_turn._MAX_ATTACHMENTS` (4) files, so uploads + `@`
  files over 4 are refused at the route (uploads alone too — they used to
  start a turn that silently read four) and the box stops the 5th with
  `READ_LIMIT_WORDS`, counting pending uploads. Enter waits while a list is
  still LOADING (it used to send the half-typed "@notes"); a message that
  never started MERGES its words and chips back into the box (old words
  first); the lists open above the card unless not even one row fits
  (`chatRefsMenuFit` `preferAbove`), never over the box; the box carries
  `aria-autocomplete` / `aria-controls` / `aria-activedescendant`. KNOWN:
  Rewind puts back the words only (not the chips or uploads); a "Resume when
  ready" continuation re-reads neither the files nor the skill; skill lists
  are read once per session mount. FONT: `@fontsource-variable/inter` (OFL,
  pinned exactly) is imported by `app/layout.tsx` (upright + `wght-italic`,
  before `globals.css`) and "Inter Variable" leads the theme's sans stack —
  the theme named Inter since the start but no file ever shipped, so
  Windows drew Segoe UI under Inter's own tracking and features
  (`cv11`/`ss01`). Self-hosted: no network at run time; each script's file
  loads only when a page uses it. LOOK: `components/harness/harnessLook.ts`
  — the transcript's rows and the bottom area (questions, queue, box) sit in
  ONE centred column, the chat page's 760 px (`HARNESS_COLUMN` is 768 =
  760 + the wrapper's 4 px padding either side, so the box lines up with the
  replies — measured), replies/your messages/the box at 15 px, and the
  Markdown renderer's compact spacing out-ranked inside a reply only
  (`[&_p]:my-3`, `[&_p]:leading-[1.7]`, `[&_ul]:space-y-1.5` …). The narrow
  Build PANE chat keeps `paneChatLook` (14 px, compact) — pinned. Pins:
  `tests/test_build_slash_at{,_review}_v1373.py`,
  `dashboard/__tests__/ij-harness-slash-at{,-review}-v1373.test.tsx`,

  `dashboard/e2e/ij-harness.spec.ts` (the 390 px `/` and `@` step; its

- **A Build session can start HELPERS, and a Build turn is HELD on its
  model, pinned account or not** (v1.372.0, IJ harness part 2 Phase 1 —
  `docs/IJ-HARNESS-PLAN-2.md`; the user's decision 4: "at most 3 at a time,

  each with the session's folder and approval setting"). ARMING:
  `harness/mode.CODING_TOOLS` gains `delegate`, `spawn_agent`, `list_agents`,

  and Codex's `spawn_agent` onto them; a `Skill` naming a FOLDER skill
  (`.ironjarvis/harness/skills/<name>`) reads its SKILL.md
  (`HarnessTurn._folder_skill`). `HELPER_DOORS` (`delegate`, `spawn_agent`)
  are armed GRANTED in the STREAM lane in every posture — the live check
  parked a "search with two helpers" turn on a card for the first helper;
  starting a helper changes nothing by itself and Claude Code / Codex never
  ask — while the user's permission table can still deny them and a folder
  rule "ask" keeps a card; the POST lane keeps the plain tier (it can show no
  card, so a helper there could wait forever on its own ask). INHERITANCE
  (`harness/helpers.py`): a `HelperParent` ContextVar is armed inside the ONE
  tool call that starts a helper (never across a `yield`); `delegate` /
  `spawn_agent` read it (`parent_for`) instead of the parent's Session row (a
  harness turn has none): the SERVED provider+model, the folder, Local only,
  low trust, the posture (`yolo` → `approve_for_me` at create), the
  conversation grants (none under `always_ask`; under Local only never a
  reaching-out tool), and the TURN'S OWN TOOLS (`helper_definition`: the
  builder gets the turn's set, read-only types its read-only part, a custom
  agent the part of its roster the turn holds; folder rules ride along; the
  runtime arms nothing on top — `arm_for_task` is skipped for
  `HELPER_ORIGIN`). A custom agent pinned to another model and a `remote:`
  agent are REFUSED in words (`target_refusal`). Origin `job:build-helper`
  (attended + asking): a helper's ask waits for the user under its own
  session id, drawn on its row (`ask` on the `helper` frame) and answered
  through `POST /chat/approvals/{id}`; the pending listing names its
  `build_session` and the bell / desktop open `/terminals?session=<id>`.
  CAP: `MAX_HELPERS` (3) per session; a 4th WAITS (`helper_slot`, row
  "waiting to start"), never refused. LIFETIME: a helper is ONE tool call —
  the call awaits its run — and the lane races helper calls against Stop
  (`until_stopped`); consecutive helper calls in one message run together
  (`_run_tool_round(together=)`); archive / delete / shutdown end the
  session's programs, whose end hook stops any helper still registered; a
  cancelled `shell` call kills its command tree at once. ROWS: `HelperCall`
  follows the helper's stream and pending asks onto the turn's side channel
  as `helper` frames (kept in the turn log, so a rejoin replays them); the
  reply line saves the settled `helpers`; the screen is
  `components/harness/HarnessHelpers.tsx` (the chat's `AgentRunRow` /
  `WorkLine`, no new styles) + `lib/buildHelpers.ts`. SKILLS: `skill_search`
  / `skill_load` are READONLY; `skill_load` returns
  `framework.guarded_instructions` (a skill that did not come from the user
  is scanned, off the loop). THE HOLD: `providers/hold.py` `ProviderHold`
  (ContextVar) is set by `start_build_turn` beside the account pin and the
  preview address (reset in reverse) with the pair the turn REALLY runs on
  (`routes/build._turn_pair`: the session's pick, else the project folder's
  default, else the configured default — the stream lane's own order; the
  dashboard's New session posts only the folder, and a hold on "" binds
  nothing), and again by `helpers.pinned` around a helper's task. It BINDS,
  not only freezes (`ModelRouter._bind_to_hold`, first thing in BOTH
  `complete()` and `stream()`): a call naming no provider — or "auto" — is
  served by the session's pair, a call naming another provider
  raises `HeldProviderRefused` (no_failover, one sentence) — an ALIAS that
  resolves to the same adapter (keyless `anthropic` → `claude-cli`) passes
  (`_same_provider`). `_session_held(auto_selected)` then keeps the
  prompted-tools wrap (same model), skips `_enforce_capabilities`, and
  raises the provider's own error before the default fallback (A) and
  sideways failover (B) — but AFTER the v1.162.0/v1.228.0 local refusal, so
  a held local model that never answered still gets "isn't connected" and
  the banner. A hold on Auto (or on the default, "") binds and holds
  nothing. Before this a Build session on this PC's own login, a local model
  or an API key could fall back to the default provider, and a picture could
  be rerouted to a vision model. Every model call a turn or helper makes
  follows the hold: `view_image` and OCR (`documents/ocr._vision_route_kwargs`)
  ask the session's model, never the vision role, and both refuse honestly
  when that model cannot see (`hold.held_model_cannot_see`) — a model that
  was never shown the picture must not describe it; a helper's compaction

  under a hold (`role_cfg`); `app._pin_armed` (the one-shot failover chain)
  answers True for a non-Auto hold. A helper is finalized INSIDE the turn,

  context copy (`core/events._drop_build_turn_scope`) — the skill-distill

  session's account and model. Under `always_ask` a helper gets an "ask"

  the runtime cards only the engine's ask tier, so with the doors granted a
  helper's writes and web calls ran uncarded. `spawn_agent` (Claude's
  `run_in_background`) refuses a custom agent pinned to another model, as
  `delegate` does; `target_refusal(manager=)` resolves an ALIAS first (a
  keyless `anthropic` agent on a `claude-cli` session is the same model).
  Same release: `test_build_sessions_v1367`'s grouping test created one
  session and appended to another inside one 15.6 ms clock tick and failed 5

  Jarvis runner answers a helper's own ask "once", like any card. Pins:

  `tests/test_harness_helpers_review_v1372.py`,
  `tests/test_build_model_hold_v1372.py`,
  `dashboard/__tests__/ij-harness-helpers{,-bell}-v1372.test.tsx`.

- **Rewind is ALL OR NOTHING through the undo journal; the library PROPOSES
  and the user accepts; the refine loop ADOPTS only into its own folder**
  (v1.370.0, Phase 7 of `docs/IJ-HARNESS-PLAN.md`; Harness² principles 6–9).
  REWIND (`harness/rewind.py`, `routes/build.rewind_session`): `GET
  /build/sessions/{id}/rewind` (checkpoints, or `?to=` a preview), `POST …
  /rewind {to}`. A turn's changes are the session's ledger rows recorded at
  or after its question's `at` (one turn runs at a time; rewinding TO a turn
  takes every later one). `plan()` SIMULATES the newest-first replay over the
  files' current bytes with each journal row's OWN "changed since" rule (its
  hash mode; a `text` row's revert re-writes newlines unless the envelope is
  `exact`) — any file that cannot go back is NAMED and nothing is touched
  (409 `problems`); only then is each action reverted through
  `routes/undo.perform_undo` (never a second copy of revert). A refusal
  between plan and revert stops at once and says what went back, leaving the
  conversation. Commands (no journal) are listed, never claimed undone. The
  rewind holds the session in `_STARTING` (no turn can start during it) and
  `sessions.cut_transcript` cuts under the conversation lock, refuses when
  the line at the cut moved, restores plan mode when the plan was approved
  in a rewound turn, and clears an account card/wait. SAME RELEASE:
  `write_file` keeps its prior copy EXACT (`make_file_descriptor(exact=)`;
  the revert writes bytes) — the old text-mode copy came back with the OS's
  line endings and made an older raw edit read as "changed since".
  LIBRARY (`harness/library.py`, `harness/library_models.HarnessLibraryRecord`
  — a late model, `routes/build_library.py`): after a build turn that FINISHED
  having changed a file or run a command (read off the ledger), ONE pinned
  router call on the turn's own model + account + Local only scope, billed to
  the thread, may mint ONE `lesson` and ONE `layer_edit` (a text file of
  `.ironjarvis/harness/`), both `proposed`. LEDGER-CHECKED (`check_lines`):
  every path must be in the SESSION's ledger (journal targets, path args,
  paths inside a command), every `"…"`/`` `…` `` span verbatim in the turn's
  transcript (question, reply, steps, the turn's ledger args + outputs); an
  unsupported line is removed; a lesson with no verified claim is not stored.
  HONEST MOCK both ways. An edit passes `validate_edit` (the coach's rules,
  +20% or +400 chars, never/always lines kept) AND Phase 3's validator on a
  scratch copy of the folder's layer; Accept stale-checks, writes atomically,
  re-validates the folder's layer and puts the file back if it is invalid.
  Only ACCEPTED lessons ride `components.compose(library=)` (its own section,
  promptguard-scanned, ≤ 10 / 2,500 chars, layers gain "library"), read in
  `chat_turn._build_harness` — before the planner. Mints go through
  `_mint_for_turn` UNDER `CONVERSATION_WRITE_LOCK` (a turn the rewind cut
  gets nothing) and a rewind deletes the PROPOSED rows of its turns
  (`forget_turns`) and cancels their reflections. Setting `build_reflect`;
  open limit 3; a decline suppresses 14 days; event `library.proposal`
  (folder NAME only). The suite turns the background reflection off for bare
  tests (`library.AUTO_REFLECT`, conftest) — a library pin turns it on.
  REFINE (`harness_bench/refine.py`, `bench-harness run --refine --k K --mode
  parallel|sequential`, `bench-harness adopt --from … --folder …`): a
  candidate is a layer folder read through `components.LAYER_SOURCE` (a
  ContextVar `_build_harness` consults; None everywhere but a probe), so the
  task copy and the hidden check are the baseline's byte for byte;
  `JarvisRunner(layer_dir=)` adds `layer_sha256` to its identity ONLY when
  set (every earlier configuration keeps its key) and records a TRAJECTORY
  (`trajectory.json`, written after the outside-folder snapshot). Evidence is
  FILES (README pointer, task.md, per-harness trajectories with the check's
  verdict + one-line reason — never the check or its output); proposals may
  write the four text files + `plugins.toml` [[rules]] only ([[checks]] run
  commands: refused), are validated by Phase 3's validator + the tripwire +
  promptguard, retried once. Winners pass ≥ the current harness on the tasks
  BOTH scored; parallel composes per file (`compose_files`), sequential takes
  the best; adoption writes `<refine>/adopted/` only. `RefineLedger` checks
  every stage's records against its rollouts, the grand totals and the
  planned budget, writes `ledger.json`, then raises. SCREEN:
  `components/harness/RewindControl.tsx` ("Rewind to here" on a message the
  daemon wrote: press one GETs the preview and says it in words, press two
  POSTs; a refusal — preview or POST 409 — is listed in the daemon's words;
  never while a reply runs; after it the session is RE-READ and the question
  goes back in the box), `LibraryCards.tsx` (Accept / Decline cards, kept
  lessons with a two-press Forget; read on open, after a reply and at
  `LIBRARY_REREAD_MS` only — never polled forever), `lib/buildRewind.ts`,
  `lib/buildLibrary.ts`; the bell maps `library.proposal` to a QUIET row
  that opens the session; e2e step "Rewind to here" on the demo model. Pins:
  `tests/test_harness_{rewind,library,refine}_v1370.py`,
  `dashboard/__tests__/ij-harness-rewind-library-v1370.test.tsx`.
  REVIEW (v1.370.0), supersedes the "after a build turn" wording above:
  THE REFLECTION IS RATIONED — on a Claude Code or Codex plan it spends the
  user's own limits. A finished turn is only NOTED (`note_turn_finished`);
  the session is reflected on ONCE it has been idle `REFLECT_IDLE_S` (600 s;
  `note_turn_started` from `start_build_turn` stops the wait) or when it is
  archived (`flush`) — ONE call over every noted turn (≤ 8,
  `stretch_evidence`; proposals carry the NEWEST turn, so a rewind to any of
  them drops it). `account_refusal` (cached reads only) skips while the
  session has a limit card / wait or Iron-Proxy says parked / signed out /
  off / ≥ `ACCOUNT_BUSY_PERCENT` (70) of a window — that stretch is KEPT for
  a later idle period. A stretch with a low-trust turn is never learned
  from; a demo turn is left out. Prompt ≤ `PROMPT_CHARS` with the notes
  first and WHOLE (`notes_shown`): an edit only for a file shown whole (a
  cut copy written back deleted the user's notes); call ≤
  `REFLECT_TIMEOUT_S`. `GET …/library` carries `reflection {due_at,
  running, turns}` and `LibraryCards` reads again only then
  (`nextLibraryRead`). The lifespan calls `library.shutdown()`. Accept
  refuses a harness folder or file that is a link (`_link_problem`) before
  any write. REWIND: the plan asks `POST /undo`'s permission engine per tool
  (a denied revert used to stop the rewind PART WAY); a row at or before
  the line before the question stays with the earlier turn (a coarse clock
  gave the previous turn's last row the question's tick); a file a rewound
  turn made that the user deleted is `already gone`, not a refusal.
  `write_file`'s `exact` copy: new rows give LF/CRLF back byte for byte,
  old rows keep the OS-newline write (`test_undo_exact_copy_v1370.py`).
  REFINE: a rubric failure says only "the answer is wrong"
  (`evidence_grade_reason`), trajectories are scrubbed of hidden-check text
  (`grader_marks`/`scrub`) and the tree goes through the tripwire
  (`HiddenCheckLeak`); a probe's budget mismatch writes the ledger first;
  reports say one run is a small sample and that "parallel" runs one at a
  time; `adopt` refuses links and missing folders. Pins:
  `tests/test_harness_{library,rewind,refine}_review_v1370.py`. SHIP STEP:
  `grading.grade` takes the hidden check files back OUT of the graded copy
  (`remove_hidden_check`) — the copy under `runs/<task>/grade/` kept the
  answer key on disk, where a later run's agent with a shell could read it;
  the agent's own work stays for inspection (`tests/test_ship_v1370.py`).
  `build_reflect` stays ON by default (one bounded call per idle stretch,
  skipped near a limit); flip it if the user wants to opt in instead.
  v1.370.1 (a USER BUG + a CI fix): "it stops right here at VR and does not
  allow me to drill down". `DirectoryTree`'s folder row was a `<button>`
  with no `type`, and the IJ harness's New session panel draws the tree
  inside its `<form>`, so every folder click SUBMITTED the form: `Users`
  answered "not writable", `VR` started a session in `C:\Users\VR`. Every
  button the tree draws is `type="button"` now (row, collapse, Open
  terminal). The sidebar's tests MOCKED the tree away, which is how it
  shipped: `harness-folder-picker-v1370-1` drives the real form with the
  real tree (both pins red on the old code). A component a FORM may contain
  never renders a typeless button. Same release: the harness's rows go side
  by side from `xl`, not `lg` (at 1024 px the conversation got ~90 px
  beside the app sidebar, the session list and the panes), pinned in
  `ij-harness-phone-unit-v1369`; and `test_harness_refine_review_v1370`
  strips Rich's colour codes before reading `--help` (v1.370.0's Tests gate
  read `\x1b[1m` between the words; `FORCE_COLOR=1 COLUMNS=80` reproduced
  it).
  v1.370.2 (the user: Build's switch "should be centered and fixed in both
  modes"): `PageHeader` takes a `center` slot. From lg it is a three-column
  grid `minmax(0,1fr) | auto | minmax(0,1fr)` (EQUAL side columns, so the
  middle is centred whatever the sides hold) whose actions column keeps
  `min-h-8` when empty; below lg title / centre / actions stack. Only Build
  passes it; every other page renders exactly as before. 41 test files MOCK
  PageHeader with `{title, actions}` only, and the 18 that render Build now
  render `center` too: a NEW slot on a widely mocked component needs its
  mocks taught, or the tests silently drop it. Pixel pin in the e2e
  (`centred and fixed`, 1280 + 1024 px: switch centre = header centre and
  unmoved between views; an un-centred grid goes red);
  `page-header-centre-v1370-2` pins the structure.

- **The IJ harness is a VIEW of Build built from the chat's own pieces; the
  daemon writes the transcript, and a turn's end is on the record BEFORE its
  last frame** (v1.369.0, Phase 6 of `docs/IJ-HARNESS-PLAN.md`). The canvas
  and `react-rnd` are gone (see the map). `components/harness/*` uses
  WorkLine / step rows / `ReplyChanges` / `TurnReceipt` / `DockAsk` (which now
  draws `ask_user` with lettered choices and the once-only `exit_plan_mode`
  card — Allow once / Deny, empty args = "the plan is the reply above") and
  `components/chat/ModelMenu` (LIFTED from the chat page; both share it). The
  screen NEVER PUTs a session's messages (only `/queue`), mints its own
  `turn_id`, rejoins a running turn, stops by turn name, and puts unread
  steer notes back in the box. Accounts: everything in `lib/buildAccounts.ts`
  (decoders; every piece hides on a 404); a pick in the chip's list sends
  PATCH with NO `continue` — `continue: true` only from the limit card; the
  session view is the truth for which account and for the card.
  `routes/build.recorded_turn` writes the reply line and the account-limit
  card (shielded, once) BEFORE passing `done`/`error` on — a re-read right
  after the last frame must see them; the account-list refresh and the bell
  event stay after. Links: a `build:<id>` row (bell, safety findings, time
  travel, `/chat/approvals/pending` `surface: "build"`) opens
  `/terminals?session=<id>`; main.js says "A Build session is waiting for
  your OK". Panes: Terminal = a normal Build terminal in the session's folder
  (parked, never `display:none`; × is two presses), Changes = `POST
  /chat/changes` with `session_id: "build:<id>"`, Browser = this PC or the
  user's network ONLY (a remembered address is re-checked on read), Files =
  `FilesPanel variant="calm"`. SAME RELEASE, shared fixes: `routes/
  chat_changes._one_change` calls a file "undone" only when every write of it
  in the window was undone and measures "changed since" against what the
  Undo left (an Undo that worked used to read "changed since · undone" + "no
  longer there", in chat too); `DirectoryTree` loads in a mount effect, never
  during render (React's "state update on a component that hasn't mounted
  yet", 3 of 10 cold /terminals loads). Below `lg` the conversation box is
  `flex-none` (a `flex-1` box took its content's height, so a long
  conversation grew the page instead of scrolling). Pins:
  `dashboard/__tests__/ij-harness-*-v1369.test.tsx`,
  `changed-files-undone-v1369`, `directory-tree-no-render-load-v1369`,
  `tests/test_{ij_harness_desktop,mock_harness_write,chat_changes_undone,
  build_turn_end_order}_v1369.py`, e2e `dashboard/e2e/ij-harness.spec.ts`.
  v1.369.1 (test-only): v1.369.0's Tests gate went red on
  `ij-harness-sidebar-v1369` ("expected null to be 'b1'"): the view writes
  the remembered session and the `?session=` address in an EFFECT after the
  commit that shows the session, and three tests waited for the screen and
  then read the address. Each now waits for the address itself; delaying the
  writes 200 ms reproduced CI's exact error on the old shape (3 red) and
  passes the new one (13/13).

- **A Build session is a chat ROW that is never a chat; its turn is the
  daemon's; plan mode asks ONCE and nothing grants it** (v1.367.0, Phase 4 of
  `docs/IJ-HARNESS-PLAN.md`). `ChatThreadRecord.surface = "build"`
  (`core/models.BUILD_SURFACE`; NULL reads as chat; `chat_surface_only()` /
  `is_build_thread()`): ONLY `POST /build/sessions` writes it. EVERY chat
  reader filters it — chat list (+project/archived), the @ menu and its
  resolver, history index (`chat_append` never indexes one; backfill skips),
  memory recall, Guide `app_search`, Status map, share (404), preferences
  look-back AND scan (`learning/preferences.py`), and
  `chat_append.append_to_chat_thread` REFUSES one (the @-panel, delivery,
  media and remote-inbound writer would otherwise land chat lines in it).
  `PUT /chat/threads/{id}` 409s; Remember / Turn into workflow 409
  (`BUILD_SESSION_NOT_A_CHAT`); the chat DELETE 409s while it answers (the
  build DELETE's `RUNNING_SENTENCE`). A NEW reader of chat threads must take
  the filter. `daemon/routes/build.py`: list (5 columns, never
  `messages_json`), create (`fs_policy.root_problem`, 400 in words), GET/
  PATCH (partial, the folder is IMMUTABLE)/DELETE, archive/unarchive (the
  chat's `archive_thread`), folder rename (`BuildFolderRecord`; a project
  root renames the PROJECT), `POST …/turn` (same SSE as /chat/stream, a
  NAMED durable turn through `TurnLogs.start`; a repeated `turn_id` is
  followed, and one whose record outlived the registry is REPLAYED from
  `ChatTurnRecord`, never re-run — 409 if it belongs to another
  conversation), `POST …/answer` (`ask_user`; checks kind AND session).
  `recorded_turn` writes the reply line in the chat page's shape, writes an
  error line when the lane raises, and applies an approved plan + "allow for
  this conversation" to the session as they land
  (`_apply_session_changes`, never raises). Both lanes take keyword-only
  `session_id=` / `plan_mode=` (never on ChatBody): tool context, ledger and
  `procs.owned_by` key on `build:<id>`; archive/delete call
  `procs.end_session`. `build_round_budget` (1..200, default 60, clamped).
  PLAN MODE arms `PLAN_MODE_TOOLS` (read-only) + `exit_plan_mode`, which
  cards ONCE-ONLY in every posture (yolo, approve-for-me, user `allow`), no
  grant covers it, Allow → act + plan kept (steps from the plan's list
  items, else the turn's last `plan_update`), Deny → plan mode stays.
  `ask_user` (allow, deadline-exempt) rides the MCP elicitation side
  channel ONLY in a harness turn that armed it (`TurnInteractions(
  asks_user=)`); anywhere else it refuses at once — the chat "+" menu can arm
  it and the chat page draws no card. Plugin checks run through `shell`, else
  `process_start(until_exit)` + `process_read` + `process_stop` (the GPT
  dialect). `/chat/approvals/pending` marks a waiting unwatched session
  `surface: "build"` (LAST key, build rows only). Boot reconcile writes ONE
  interrupted line per cut-off turn (honouring `reset`). Pins:
  `tests/test_build_{sessions,turns,plan_ask,checks,review,leftovers}_v1367.py`.
  SAME RELEASE, A REAL HARM FROM THE SUITE: `iron_proxy.service._CURRENT`
  is process-global (every `IronProxyService(register=True)` sets it), so a
  test that switched Iron-Proxy on left it on for later tests on the same
  worker, and that service found the USER'S running proxy through
  `~/.iron-proxy/proxy.json`. Claude-native tests then leased the user's
  real accounts, and a scripted 401 ("Invalid bearer token") was SIGNALLED
  to it — the live "TSI" account read "needs to sign in again" (repaired
  with the card's Check again). `tests/conftest.py` now gives every worker
  a throwaway `IRON_PROXY_DATA_DIR` and restores `_CURRENT` after each
  test. A NEW process-global that can reach the user's live services gets
  the same two fixtures.

- **Harness mode: one switch, a dialect per model family, a component tree
  per folder** (v1.366.0, Phase 3 of `docs/IJ-HARNESS-PLAN.md`; Harness²
  principles 1–3). `harness=` is KEYWORD-ONLY on `run_chat_turn` /
  `stream_chat_turn` / `chat_stream` (True/"auto"/a dialect id; else
  ValueError before work; never on ChatBody); OFF IS BYTE-IDENTICAL, pinned
  by a golden fixture captured from the pre-change code
  (`tests/fixtures/harness_off_golden_v1366.json`, `IJ_REGEN_HARNESS_OFF=1`)
  and a pin that makes every harness entry point raise with harness off.
  ON: arms `harness/mode.CODING_TOOLS` past the 6-named-tool cap (allow tier
  armed+granted; ask tier visible+carded in the stream lane; the POST lane
  arms no ask-tier tool), renders the component tree BEFORE the history
  planner, never escalates (exit lines swapped, roster dropped, invented exits
  refused, `stay_in_chat`, 60 rounds), and puts `route.harness = {family,
  dialect, fingerprint, layers, nudged[, note]}` LAST on both lanes.
  DIALECTS (`harness/dialects.py`): claude = Claude Code's shapes, gpt =
  Codex's (`exec_command` → `process_start(until_exit)`, else shell), qwen =
  Qwen Code @13a4fea5, generic = Jarvis's names; the family is read off the
  serving provider + model id; `FAMILY_MEASURED` records the evidence — glm
  maps to GENERIC because the bench measured it (claude dialect: equal pass
  rate, ~40% more tool calls, ~73% more input tokens); deepseek/qwen "not yet
  measured". TRANSLATION at the boundary (`HarnessTurn.translate_calls`,
  right after the router answers): every gate (cards, repeat guard, spend
  check, low trust, `registry.invoke(allowed_names=)`) sees the REAL tool;
  `ToolInvocation.called_as` (additive; the reconciler adds it) records the
  model's name; the model gets its own names back; what is armed is exactly
  what the model is shown. COMPONENTS (`harness/components.py`): the eight
  Harness² components, layers default → family → `<folder>/.ironjarvis/harness/`
  (omitted-is-inherited); `DEFAULT_GUARDRAILS` is a FLOOR (folder, never
  stop what you did not start, never end processes by name, destructive git
  only when asked) and `FLOOR_CLOSING` ends the list whenever the folder's
  own files speak; the validator voids a whole invalid layer with one note
  (unknown files, size caps, junction/symlink escapes, untriggered skills,
  missing scripts, plugins.toml schema — rules only ask/deny, a tool named
  twice voids it); every folder text through promptguard, a flagged check
  voids the layer; checks run as GATED `shell` calls ledgered `called_as
  "plugin check"`. TEXT TOOL CALLS (GLM `<tool_call>`, `<invoke name=…>`,
  a JSON blob naming an offered tool) are never executed: ONE nudge per turn
  (not when no tools were offered), the stream lane sends `reset`. Bench:
  `--dialect auto|generic|claude|gpt|qwen`, identity includes dialect, family
  and fingerprint. Pins: `tests/test_harness_{components,dialects,mode_lanes,
  off_golden,phase3_review}_v1366.py`.

- **The coding tools Claude Code and Codex give their models, on Jarvis's
  own registry** (v1.364.0, Phase 1 of `docs/IJ-HARNESS-PLAN.md`). The parity
  table `src/iron_jarvis/harness/parity.py` maps EVERY tool of Claude Code
  2.1.306 (46) and Codex 0.157.0 (43) — inventory fixture
  `tests/fixtures/harness_tool_inventory_v1364.json` — to the Jarvis tool that
  serves it, "later (Phase 4)" or "not needed: <reason>"; a pin fails on an
  unmapped inventory name. Tools (package `src/iron_jarvis/harness/`:
  textedit, patch, verify, globmatch, search, tools, process): `read_file`
  `offset`/`limit`/`numbered` (cat -n; with NONE of them the output is
  byte-identical to before); STRICT `edit_file` (0 matches refused with the
  nearest line, >1 refused with the count, `replace_all`; LF/CRLF/BOM kept,
  raw undo — it used to turn LF into CRLF on Windows); `multi_edit` (one
  file, all or nothing); `apply_patch` (Codex V4A, Codex's matching ladder,
  multi-file, validated before any write, a failed write rolls back EVERY
  file incl. the one that failed; undo kind `files_patch` read by outcome,
  mission team docs, world, chat_changes and prune_events); `glob`; `grep`
  (with only pattern/path the ORIGINAL code runs byte for byte; any new arg
  switches to the new engine); `shell` `timeout_s` (1..600) + `cwd` on the
  REGISTERED `SandboxedShellTool`; `process_start/read/write/stop`
  (`procs.spawn` in a kill-on-close Job, per-session table, cap 4 reserved
  ATOMICALLY (`ProcessTable.reserve` — a parallel step started 7 under a cap
  of 4), 1 MB ring, stopped by a `procs.add_end_hook` that `end_session` and
  `end_all` run); `plan_update`; `notebook_edit`;
  `mcp_list_resources/mcp_read_resource`; a one-line "Check:" after a code
  write (py/json/ipynb/toml, js via `node --check` when present; never fails
  the write). Registrations: permission rows (process_start/write ask, on
  the deny floor, LOW_TRUST_DENY, RUNS_COMMANDS_TOOLS; process_read/stop
  allow — they reach only the session's own programs), egress
  `READS_APP_RESOURCES_TOOLS`, `_MUTATING_TOOLS`, detections, run words +
  dashboard twins, card words, `STRICT_ASK_TOOLS` (Ask before file edits cards
  the new editors) and the chat lanes' documents list / honesty note
  (`chat_turn.reported_document_paths`, `_FILE_CHANGING_TOOLS`, lock-step).
  OFF every roster (`_OFF_ROSTER_BY_DESIGN`) and never auto-armed: the IJ
  harness arms them (Phase 3/4). OPEN for Phase 4: the process tools key on
  `ctx.session_id` and every chat turn is session "chat" — a build turn must
  run under its own session id and end it. Pins: `tests/test_harness_tools_*_v1364.py`,
  `tests/test_harness_phase1_review_v1364.py`.

- **The IJ harness is MEASURED: `ironjarvis bench-harness`** (v1.363.0, Phase
  0 of `docs/IJ-HARNESS-PLAN.md`; Harness² principles 4–6, 10 — ideas only).
  `src/iron_jarvis/harness_bench/`: 20 tasks (`tasks/<id>/` = `task.md`, the
  `repo/` the agent gets, a HIDDEN `check/` marked `IJ-BENCH-HIDDEN-CHECK`,
  `task.toml` with category/check/timeout or a deterministic `[rubric]` over
  an `answer.json` — never a model call), reference solutions in
  `tests/fixtures/harness_bench_solutions/` (pinned: every untouched repo FAILS
  its check, every solution PASSES). Runners `jarvis` (the STREAM chat lane
  with an explicit `round_budget` + `stay_in_chat` — keyword-only on
  `run_chat_turn`/`stream_chat_turn`/`chat_stream`, never on `ChatBody`,
  validated by `turn_round_budget()` (1..`MAX_TURN_ROUNDS`=200); omitted =
  every existing lane byte-identical, pinned), `claude` (`claude -p
  --safe-mode --output-format stream-json … --permission-mode
  bypassPermissions` in the disposable copy, env = `claude_models.child_env` +
  the session-marker strip, so cost is always list-price-equivalent) and
  `codex` (`codex exec --json … --dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox
  --ignore-user-config` — on Windows `--sandbox workspace-write` rejected
  EVERY Codex command "blocked by policy" and the first baseline scored Codex
  0/3 for it; a refusal like that is now "did not run", via
  `runners.codex_sandbox_refusals` over Codex's own ERROR lines minus the
  prompt echo; billing from `OPENAI_API_KEY`/`CODEX_API_KEY` or `codex login
  status` words, never the status text). Every child through `core/procs`.
  TIERS: `core` (20; a task.toml with no tier is core — their hashes stay
  stable) and `hard` (10, each with a pinned DECOY in
  `tests/fixtures/harness_bench_decoys/` that passes the visible tests and
  fails the hidden check); `--tier core|hard|all`; reports give pass rate per
  tier. THE RULES: the hidden check
  runs in a separate grading copy AFTER the run and a tripwire refuses a
  workspace or prompt that carries it; a check that cannot run is UNSCORED
  (exit 0 pass, 1 fail, else unscored) and leaves the denominator — but a
  check that runs past its own time limit is a FAIL ("the code it tests did
  not finish"): unscoring it let an agent's hang raise the pass rate;
  `failures.classify_failure` marks a run "did not run" (unscored, no marker,
  rerun next time) ONLY on positive evidence — a runner fact (CLI missing,
  start failed, demo model, failover, tripwire) or infrastructure words in the
  runner's own error — while the agent's own time limit, round/turn cap or a
  stop after it `acted` is GRADED (the first cut defaulted the other way and
  inflated pass rates; the reviewer caught it); a completion marker carries a
  fingerprint (runner, model, task hash, prompt/tool/profile identity,
  version) and a run is reused only with a matching marker and an unchanged
  workspace; a budget ledger must equal each record; `--seed`/`--dry-run`,
  one folder per configuration under `<home>/harness-bench/`, a mismatched
  resume refused in words; the app's own home, `IRONJARVIS_HOME` and any
  folder inside the source checkout are refused as `--root`. Every runner's
  prompt carries `runners.GUARDRAIL` (work only in this folder; never stop a
  program you did not start; never end processes by name) — a review run's
  model finished a task with `taskkill /f /im python.exe` and killed every
  Python program on the PC. The Jarvis runner ends the lane's "chat" process
  session after every turn (`procs.end_session`). Reports split "did not run"
  from "check could not run", list killed runs as interrupted, and say the
  "outside" column watches only the bench's folder. NOT in CI (it spends
  model usage); the suite drives it with a scripted model and fake CLIs. Pins:
  `tests/test_harness_bench_v1363.py`, `tests/test_chat_round_budget_v1363.py`.
  v1.370.4: the full 30-task comparison ran (results in
  `docs/IJ-HARNESS-PLAN.md`) and found the ledger comparing money at 1e-9
  while the chat lane rounds a turn's cost to 6 places — an honest record
  read "the run says 0.065993, the ledger says 0.0659935". `ledger.differences
  (..., records=N)` compares money within `MONEY_ROUNDING_USD` (half a
  millionth) per record summed; tokens stay exact; `verify_plan` and the
  refine ledger pass their record counts. Pin:
  `tests/test_bench_ledger_rounding_v13704.py`. Known gap: a record names the
  model only as "default"/"subscription", not the model that answered.

- **A coding session is drawn like the chat page: never redrawn per word, a
  reply that never blinks, the finish check off the live reply, drafts per
  session** (v1.417.0, fix plan C2.1/C2.2/C2.4/C2.7). RENDERS:
  `useChatStream({clockInState: false})` keeps "when the turn last moved" out
  of the caller's state (it lives in `lastEventStore`; the default keeps the
  old state for the chat page's QuietNote) — HarnessSession passes it beside
  `textInState: false`, so a token redraws only LiveText. Each message is
  `components/harness/HarnessMessageRow` (memo; plain values + ONE `h`
  handlers object built once from a ref, `undoFor`/`onUndo` passed as they
  are because receipts call them while rendering; `prevUserAts` gives each
  reply its changes window); `HarnessPanes`, `WorkLine` and `TurnReceipt`
  are `memo`; the panes get `livePlan` memoised on `stream.tools`, stable
  `onSessionChanged`/`onCompact`, a memoised `currentAccount`; CodeView's
  terminal API is `useMemo`; IjHarnessView's list poll keeps the old array
  when `sameFolders` (structural). A test's `useDaemon`/`useModels` mock must
  hand back CONSTANT objects or every memo is defeated (the real hooks are
  stable). SETTLE: `held` (the turn that ended) keeps `harness-live` up until
  `shown` holds that turn's reply or `settle` gave up — the swap is ONE
  render; LiveText's `settling` drops the Thinking line; the next-step box
  waits too. FOLLOW: LiveText calls `onGrow` per published growth;
  `followLive` sets the transcript's `scrollTop` (never scrollIntoView — the
  page must not move) only while `followRef` (within `FOLLOW_SLACK_PX` 80 of
  the bottom, read on scroll). FINISH: `sseEventFrom` decodes `check:
  "finish"` on token/thinking/reset/round/tool_call and the done frame's
  `finish` record (raw); `run()` folds those frames into `finishStore`
  (`LiveFinish {running, test, record}`) and NEVER into the text, the cards,
  the thinking or `onToken`; HarnessSession's `LiveFinishLine`
  (`useLiveFinish` in lib/liveThinking — not exported from useChatStream, so
  no mock changes) says `FINISH_CHECKING_WORDS`, the test run
  (`FINISH_TEST_WORDS`), then `finishWords` of the record. The daemon half
  (tagging the round's frames) is fix-plan lane A; the chat page never gets
  `check` and is unchanged. DRAFTS: `lib/chatDrafts` under `draftKeyOf(id)`
  (`build:<id>`): read at mount, written at unmount and `pagehide`, cleared
  at send/queue; the box autofocuses once when the view arrives (not on a
  hidden split column, not with a card up, never over a field being typed
  in); it grows via `boxHeightFor` up to `BOX_MAX_ROWS` (8) lines,
  `harnessLook` untouched. New `useChatStream` exports: TYPES only
  (`ValueStore`, `LiveFinish`). Pins: `dashboard/__tests__/harness-render-
  discipline-v1417.test.tsx` (counts session/row/view renders through
  pass-through stubs), `harness-live-settle-v1417.test.tsx`. REVIEW: the
  panes' memo was unpinned (a pass-through `HarnessTimeline` on the Timeline
  tab now counts HarnessPanes' renders: 0 over 30 tokens and four keystrokes);
  so was the hold's RELEASE (a settle read that finds no saved reply, or
  cannot read the session, must take the live block down — pinned both
  ways); the settle test held its read on a 400 ms TIMER and went red under a
  loaded run (the read answered before the checks) — it holds the read on a
  gate the test releases; `finishTag` had orphaned `sseEventFrom`'s doc
  comment. Known: while the live block is held the "Working for N s" line
  keeps counting (≤ the settle read).

- **Chat › Code starts at the composer, reads a reply IN ORDER, has ONE line
  under each reply, and lists its archived sessions** (v1.418.0, fix plan
  C2.3/C2.5/C2.6/C2.8, wave 2 lane E). START (C2.6):
  `components/harness/HarnessStartComposer.tsx` is the empty view AND New
  session / Ctrl+N / a folder's "+" / "/clear" (IjHarnessView `middle ===
  "new"`, `openNew(folder)`): a focused box + chips (FolderChip
  `harness-new-folder`, the branch switch only when `fetchFolderGit` says
  repo + canBranch, PermissionChip `harness-new-posture`, a model select,
  Local only). NOTHING exists on the daemon until Send: ONE `POST
  /build/sessions` with `startCreateBody` (only what the user chose), then
  `keepView(made)` + HarnessSession `freshView` (the create's own view: NO
  mount GET) + `firstMessage` (sent once via `send(item)`, `onFirstTaken`).
  The v1.412.0 "make an empty session at once" path and its abandoned-session
  clean-up in IjHarnessView are GONE (nothing to abandon); the in-session
  `HarnessStartFolder` move stays for an empty session that already exists.
  IN ORDER (C2.3): `lib/replyItems.ts` — live, the frame tap
  (`onFrame` tool_call, not `check: "finish"`) records the text store's length
  per card in `liveOffsetsFor(turnId)` (a frame is tapped before the next is
  read, so the store holds exactly the earlier words; LiveText also records a
  card it first sees at render as a fallback) and `liveReplyItems` splits the
  words there; earlier runs are `WorkLine.StepRun` (folded, `runWords`), the
  current run `LiveToolRows`, `harness-live-text` = the LAST text item
  (`harness-live-text-part` the earlier ones). The daemon saves only the LAST
  round's words (`done.reply`), so a settled reply is placed by the shape this
  window kept (`keepReplyShape`, ≤ 32): earlier words, saved steps by count,
  then the saved content; not watched here = steps then content. Runs leave
  out `check: "finish"` steps and `helper` steps. ONE LINE (C2.5):
  `HarnessMessageRow.ReplyLine` (`reply-line`, `reply-line-toggle`,
  `replyLineParts`: Worked for · N steps · Checked · N failed · cut off) with
  TurnReceipt inline (its warnings stay visible) and Branch on the same row;
  the panel holds WorkLine (`timing={null}`: "Worked for" is said once),
  SettledPlan, ReplyChanges and the newest reply's `next-step-recap`
  (HarnessSession computes `nextStep` once; the NextStepBox gets `recap: ""`
  and keeps its buttons and follow-up chips). ARCHIVE (C2.8):
  `listArchivedSessions` (GET `?archived=1`, read at mount and after a change,
  never on the poll), HarnessSidebar `ArchivedFold` ("Archived (N)", rows,
  `harness-bring-back-<id>`), the session's `harness-archived-banner` +
  `harness-bring-back`; a row's Bring back re-reads an OPEN session
  (`rereadRequest`). Pins: `dashboard/__tests__/ij-harness-{start-composer,
  reply-items}-v1418.test.tsx`; moved on purpose: `ij-harness-abandoned-v1412`,
  `ij-harness-new-folder-v1378`, `ij-harness-new-folder-nofolder-review-v1379`,
  `ij-harness-phase6-v1377` (Ctrl+N), `ij-harness-phase7-review-v1378`,
  `ij-harness-sidebar-v1369`, `finish-check-v1386`, `ij-harness-keys-review-
  v1377`, `ij-harness-session-v1369`, `ij-harness-work-v1374`,
  `next-step-v1406`, `session-team-v1403`, e2e `ij-harness{,-git,
  -attempts}.spec.ts` (their `startInFolder` types and sends). REVIEW (each
  red first, `ij-harness-{start-composer,reply-items}-review-v1418`): (1) A
  FIRST MESSAGE REFUSED BEFORE ITS TURN STARTED (a 400 on POST …/turn) left
  the session the start had just made as an EMPTY row — the removed clean-up
  used to be the only thing that took such rows away. Now HarnessSession's
  `onFirstRefused` (only while `firstPendingRef`: from the hand-over until the
  turn's first frame or the send ends) hands the words back: IjHarnessView
  `firstRefused` deletes that row with `only_if_empty` and reopens the
  composer with `seed` (the words, the files, every chip, the refusal) — as
  if nothing had been made; a second message's refusal stays in its session
  as before. (2) Ctrl+N / New session / a folder's "+" while the composer is
  ALREADY open remounted it and dropped the words and the folder: now `again`
  (`StartAgain {n, folder}`) keeps them, sets the folder given and puts the
  caret back; a seed or press is forgotten once the view shows anything else
  (never handed to a later composer). (3) A refusal not about the folder was
  said in red ON THE FOLDER CHIP: `isFolderProblem` routes folder/branch/
  repository/worktree words to the chip, the rest to `harness-new-problem`.
  (4) THE TAP COUNTS THE WORDS ITSELF (`tapLenRef`: tokens added, `reset`
  zeroes it and moves every step placed so far to 0, `resume {resumed:false}`
  zeroes it, a resumed run starts from its seed's length): the text store
  stands still through a replay (every replayed step landed above every
  word), and after a `reset` (a cut-off call, the text-tool-call nudge, the
  language rewrite) an old place cut the NEW words mid-word. (5) A kept
  segment the saved content already holds (the approved plan put before the
  reply, a closing offer's combined answer) is drawn once. OPEN: the
  in-session `HarnessStartFolder` (an empty session opened by address) and
  `e2e/build-code-start.spec.ts`'s 390 px pin of it are unchanged; a first
  message that is only a "/" command (`/model`, `/clear`) makes a session
