- **A terminal's output reaches a model only through `_fence_terminal_output`, "Type it in" types one line, and a reset more than eight days away is not believed** (v1.520.0, third review TM1, TM14, TM15).
  `routes/terminals._fence_terminal_output` scans (`detect_injection`) then
  fences (`wrap_untrusted`); both pane routes use it and add
  `TERMINAL_DATA_RULE`. A scan hit withholds the output: "ask AI" says so
  ahead of its answer (`TERMINAL_WITHHELD_NOTE`, `withheld: true`), and
  "make a workflow from this terminal" answers 422 before any model call.
  The scan test in `tests/test_third_plan_g17_terminal_fence.py` lists every
  reader of `output_tail` / `scrollback_bytes` (`_READERS`): a new reader
  fails it until it is fenced or shown not to reach a model.
  `TerminalPane.oneLineCommand`: "Type it in" shows for one line and types
  it with no line break; more lines get Copy. (The Build page's "Run in
  terminal" still sends every line: there the press is the consent.)
  `limit_state.MAX_RESET_AHEAD` (8 days): a further reset parses to None
  and is not passed on in `signal_text`; the limit itself still stands.

- **"Try on…" carries a folder's uncommitted files through the night commit's own screen** (v1.520.0, third review NA5).
  `attempts.screened_tree` calls `night_commit.judge_changes` /
  `parse_diff_raw` (public since this release; do not copy the patterns).
  A file that fails is not staged (a tracked one is carried at its committed
  copy), `_Unscreened` refuses the start when the screen cannot be sure, and
  the card's `left_out` names each file (`left_out_line`,
  `HarnessAttempts.leftOutLine`). Keep never touches the user's copy. Left
  open: a session that works in its folder directly, after Keep, is in a
  folder without the left-out file (said on the card and in the keep note).
  Pin: `tests/test_third_plan_g17_attempts_screen.py`.

- **A coding reply that was stopped, or that had ended, is never carried on by the start-up pass; a finished reply never gets a limit card; Delete holds the session** (v1.522.0, third review RB1, RB2, RB5, RB8, NA8).
  `routes/build.resume_refusal` asks `stop_was_asked` FIRST and
  `reply_ended` (the session already holds a reply line for the turn that
  is not a cut-off line). A stopped reply is kept by `keep_stopped_reply`:
  its line says `sessions.STOPPED_LINE` with `canContinue` when rounds were
  kept, and the user's "Continue from where it stopped" sends
  `STOPPED_RESUME_NOTE`. A watch cancelled AFTER its done or error frame
  records done or error, never "interrupted". In `recorded_turn` the
  account pin's limit counts only when the terminal chunk is not `done`
  (a side call's limit must not arm a wait on finished work), and
  `_keep_account_limit` arms no wait for a reply whose stop was asked.
  `DELETE /build/sessions/{id}` is async, holds `_STARTING` and
  `_DELETING` for its whole run (the slow part in a thread), and is busy
  while `_running_turn` says a turn's log is still running (an attempts
  turn writes its card after its last lane left). Left open: the stopped
  line is drawn in the error tone (`HarnessFailure.tsx`);
  `keep_stopped_reply` calls the private `reply_record._move_failed`.
  Pins: `tests/test_third_plan_g19_coding_stop.py`,
  `dashboard/__tests__/third-plan-g19-coding-stop.test.tsx`.

- **One reading of an attempts card decides a night try-on task's ending, its usage and its limits; the night's "at once" setting bounds its attempts** (v1.523.0, third review NA1, NA2, NA3, NA4, NA6, NA7, TM13).
  `attempts.card_reading(raw)` (through `night.attempts_reading(line)`):
  an attempt is finished only in state "done"; one or more finished =
  NEEDS_YOU (note kind `attempts`), none = FAILED with each attempt's
  ending; `night.outcome_of` applies it, so such a task is never DONE.
  `night_morning.usage_by_account` adds each attempt's usage from the same
  reading. At night `check_target(..., every_pick=True)` checks pay per use
  for EVERY pick, the default included; a refused pick becomes a card row
  (`attempts.not_started`, "Did not start: ...") and the others run; none
  startable refuses the request. `Plan.pay_room` is the night's remaining
  dollars and `SharedCap(pay_usd=)` counts all pay-per-use attempts against
  that ONE remainder. A subscription pick whose accounts could not be read
  is refused (`_accounts_unread`). `Plan.max_running` (night only) is a
  semaphore in `run_attempts`; `night.task_weight` is `min(picks, at
  once)`; an attempt's time limit counts from `Attempt.wall_start`, and the
  overrun backstop allows one limit per round (`overrun_after_s`). By day
  nothing changes: all picks start together and any refusal refuses the
  request. `terminals/agent_state`: npm's echo of a script (`> name@version
  script` and the `>` line under it) is not an idle prompt
  (`_script_echo_rows`). Left open: a waiting attempt's row says
  "starting"; an echo with no `@version` is not recognised.
  Pins: `tests/test_third_plan_g21_night_attempts.py`,
  `tests/test_third_plan_g21_screenshot_and_idle.py`.

- **Continue uses the account the page names, a "with tools" launch is set up before it says ready, a stricter level reaches the running reply, and the session's record decides its boxes** (v1.524.0, third review TM4, TM5, TM16, RB3, RB14, MC1, MC6).
  `continue_on.prepare(..., account=)`: `_named_choice` returns a lease for
  exactly that account or raises `ContinueRefused(instead=)`, BEFORE any
  signal or copy; no refusal can follow the signal (a failed `pick` falls
  back to the account read before it). No account named keeps the daemon's
  own pick. `POST /terminals/launch-with-tools` makes the terminal with its
  boxes, `recipe_required=True` (`RecipeNotSetUp` before the spawn, the
  token revoked) and types the start command plus the recipe's `argv_extra`
  (`_shell_args` quotes per shell); `TerminalManager._prepare_recipe` asks
  the preparer's dry run (`inspect`) before it mints a token; a recipe row
  carries `ready`. `routes/build.LevelWatch` (a ContextVar set by
  `start_build_turn`): the stream lane reads the session's level once per
  round, off the loop, and `tighten()` takes it only when it changed since
  the reply started AND is stricter; Plan switched on mid-reply refuses
  every call outside the plan tools through the deny path
  (`PLAN_NOW_REFUSAL`). The PATCH is async and answers `applies` (`"now"` /
  `"next_reply"`) and `applies_local_only` only while a reply runs. A branch
  copies `cap`. `hosted/adopt._adopt_one` sets `live.caps` from the
  session's record, and the capabilities PATCH calls `snapshot_now()`.
  RB4 was not a defect: the one writer of a Local only hold refuses outside
  chat, so no coding session ever has one. Left open: Local only switched on
  mid-reply does not reach the running reply (the PATCH says so; an open
  session has no Local only switch); the typed start line was not run
  against a real coding tool on the packaged build.
  Pins: `tests/test_third_plan_g20_terminals.py`,
  `tests/test_third_plan_g20_session_settings.py`,
  `tests/test_third_plan_g20_door_card.py`, and the three
  `dashboard/__tests__/third-plan-g20-*.test.tsx` files.

- **A terminal survives a restart or says why it did not: one reader per session, a bounded second start, a folder check before any process, and backups without its output** (v1.526.0, third review TM2, TM3, TM6 to TM12, TM17 to TM19).
  `backend.check_start_folder` runs first in every backend's `start`
  (bounded; `StartFolderGone` is a `ShellStartStuck`, answered 400).
  `session.restart_at_size` starts the new shell on its own thread within
  `RESTART_START_WAIT_S`; until it has started the OLD shell keeps the
  screen and the keys, and a failed start leaves it (`RESTART_FAILED_NOTE`,
  `restart_wanted()`). `pane_spawn` calls `start_autodrain()` (one reader,
  under `_drain_lock`; a polled backend had none until a page attached).
  `manager.rehydrate` keeps a terminal that could not be started with its
  reason (`restore_failed_panes`, `retry_restore`, `forget_restore`, the
  two `/terminals/not-reopened/{id}` routes, `NotReopenedList.tsx`); the
  cap counts a create in flight once (`_in_use_locked`,
  `SessionCapReached` is the only 429). `_pty_ok` is retried after
  `_PTY_RETRY_S`, and a shell that `ended_by_itself()` does not mark the
  engine broken. The shutdown snapshot names the running program and
  `restore` adds one line under the old output. `maintenance.create_backup`
  leaves out `terminals-scrollback/` and every `scrollback_b64`.
  `continue_on.carry_over` copies the conversation and its side folder as
  two steps. Continue and start-fresh publish `terminal.continued` /
  `terminal.started_fresh` (never the conversation's text). The assist's
  reads and a resize run off the loop (`_off_loop`). Left open: after a
  crash no program is named; `rehydrate` still drops snapshot entries past
  the session cap.
  Pins: `tests/test_third_plan_g22_terminal_engine.py`,
  `tests/test_third_plan_g22_terminals_restart.py`,
  `dashboard/__tests__/third-plan-g22-terminals.test.tsx`.

- **A "Resume when ready" carry-on that is refused ends its wait and says why; the question and the wait's removal are ONE write; a grant that cannot be saved is kept and said** (v1.527.0, third review RB6, RB7, RB11, RB13).
  `routes/build._wait_refused` (from `resume_due`): a lasting refusal
  (`wait_refusal_passes` False) ends the wait with `S.amend_wait` (only that
  same wait) and fills `_WAIT_ENDED`, shown as the view's
  `account_limit.wait_ended`; a passing one (5xx, 429, Compact, a delete,
  Emergency stop, accounts not listed) is retried until `WAIT_GIVE_UP_S`.
  `S.take_wait(lines=)` saves the carry-on's question in the same locked
  write that takes the wait; a write that fails raises and changes nothing
  (503 `QUESTION_NOT_SAVED`, never 404). `append_lines` answers
  `failed: True` for a failed write of a session that exists.
  `RR.drop_failed` runs only after the question is saved, and
  `_not_started` moves the kept rounds `RR.claim` took back to their key.
  RB11: `_session_write_due` spaces the session write after a failure
  (`SESSION_WRITE_RETRY_S`, the injectable `_session_clock`), the log says
  it once then a line a minute, and `_keep_unsaved_grants` keeps a grant in
  `_UNSAVED_GRANTS` for the next reply (`GRANT_NOT_SAVED` on the last
  frame). `folder_problem` answers `FOLDER_CHECK_FAILED` through
  `plain_failure` when the check raises. `_UNSAVED_GRANTS` lives in memory:
  a restart forgets it (why a wait ended and when its refusal began are
  stored since v1.529.0, see below).
  Pins: `tests/test_third_plan_g23_coding_routes.py`,
  `dashboard/__tests__/third-plan-g23-wait-ended.test.tsx`.

- **An open Chat › Code session's Local only is its own switch in the
  session's ⋯ menu, and a held session never turns it off** (v1.529.0, owner

  the existing PATCH `local_only`; while a reply runs the toolbar says it
  applies from the next message (`applies_local_only`). A session held by an
  app kept on the user's own models (`local_only_holds`) shows it on, sends

  `dashboard/__tests__/session-local-only-v1529.test.tsx`.

- **A Build › Code reply the tool ended at the model's length limit ends
  "failed" with `cut: "length"`, never "completed"** (v1.529.0, owner
  decision 5, third plan RB10). ACP `max_tokens` (`hosted/acp.stop_of`),
  Claude Code's result `stop_reason: "max_tokens"` and Pi's last answer
  `stopReason: "length"` all end through `events.make_done(stop, cut)` with
  `words.OUTPUT_LIMIT_CUT` said once; a Stop pressed meanwhile is a Stop.
  This matches a Chat › Code reply's `truncated`
  (`finished_notice_outcome`). The bell's check mark is for outcome "done"
  only ("limit" is the pause sign, "failed" and "needs_you" the warning);
  the activity list says "Stopped at its limit". Codex reports no length

- **Why a "Resume when ready" wait ended (`account_limit.wait_ended`) and
  when its carry-on was first refused for a passing reason
  (`account_wait.refused_at`) are stored with the session, never in daemon
  memory** (v1.529.0, owner decision 7, third plan RB6). Written through
  `S.amend_wait(..., None, ended=)` in the same write that ends the wait,
  and kept by `routes/chat._ACCOUNT_LIMIT_KEYS` / `_ACCOUNT_WAIT_KEYS`
  (`wait_ended` up to 600 characters); `S.forget_wait_ended` clears the
  words when a reply starts or a new wait is armed. The bell still does not
  ring when a wait ends. Pin: `tests/test_wait_ended_kept_v1529.py`.

- **The "Other tools" part folds to its heading, and the choice is kept per
  place** (v1.531.0, the owner: "in Chat › Code I can't minimize the other
  tools"). The heading `other-tools-fold` (`aria-expanded`) hides every tool
  row, offer and note; folded, it says how many sessions work (Chat › Code)
  or are new (`other-tools-folded-note`). Kept in this browser under
  `lib/otherTools.FOLDED_KEY` as `{chat, code}`, so Chat › Code and the chat
  list each keep their own; open until folded. Pin:
  `dashboard/__tests__/other-tools-fold-v1531.test.tsx`.

- **A coding reply that ended on its done frame without finishing is read in ONE place, and an attempt follows the session's level** (v1.534.0, fourth review CS1-CS5, owner decisions D12 and D4 A).
  `harness/reply_end.cut_short(d)` returns "length" (`truncated`), "cut_off" (an unrecovered `route.cut_off`) or "" for a done frame or a reply line; `CUT_WORDS` are its words. The night (`outcome_of`, before any limit), attempts (`Attempt.settle`, before cap and step limit), the day notice (`finished_notice_outcome`), `plan_run_due` and `sessions.reply_end_note` all read it; a scan in `tests/test_fourth_review_reply_end_v1534.py` fails any other reader of those keys in the five files (one allowed copy in `_ReplyWatch.lines`). A cut night task or attempt is FAILED; its work is still committed or keepable. The dashboard reads it only through `lib/replyEnd.cutShort`, whose words are parsed from the Python file (`dashboard/__tests__/reply-end-twin-v1534.test.ts`). An attempts turn's notice comes from its card (`card_reading`): "choose" ("Your attempts are ready: choose the one to keep") when one or more finished, "failed" when none did; the daemon, `comm/notifier.build_finished_title`, the tray (`main.js runOutcomeNotice`) and the bell (`buildFinishedHeadline`, the `toActivity` icon, `eventWords.finishedDetail`) change together, pinned by the `build-turn-choose` case of `one-notice-v13600.json`. A stopped attempts turn (done frame `stopped`) is recorded "stopped" and sends no notice. A met cost cap set by the month's pay-per-use remainder carries `of: "pay_left"` BESIDE `reply_cap.clean()`, never through it. `maybe_start` arms one `LevelWatch` per attempt (`routes/build.arm_level_watch`, read with `getattr` so an older plan object has none), and the stream lane's done frame says `plan_now`. The Last night row reads a tried-on-several-models task's card through `GET /build/sessions/{id}` and offers Keep where the card's one rule (`lib/buildAttempts.keepOffered`) does.

- **A stop names who made it, on the step, the reply's line, its Continue note and the night's ending** (v1.535.0, fourth review CS6/DT4).
  `harness/stall.stop_step(turn_id, call_id, *, by=)` and the reply's Stop carry the author; the ledger row, the model's result (`STOPPED_BY_WORDS`, read through a context the step runner sets) and the finish check's "not run" words (`finish.stopped_why`) name the time limit, Emergency stop or nobody, never "you" unless the user pressed it. The night backstop passes `STOP_BY_TIME_LIMIT`, Emergency stop `STOP_BY_EMERGENCY`, the night's Cancel `STOP_BY_YOU`. A stopped coding reply's line (`sessions.stopped_line(by)`, with `stoppedBy`), the resume pass's refusal (`STOPPED_REFUSALS`), the Continue note (`STOPPED_RESUME_NOTES`) and the history note follow the author; the user's own words are unchanged byte for byte. The night's `outcome_of` reads an interrupted line whose `stoppedBy` is not "you" as STOPPED. `agents/fix_request._STOP_MARKERS` holds every `STOPPED_BY_WORDS` phrase, so a step stopped by a limit is never read as a defect. Pins: `tests/test_fourth_review_stop_steps_v1535.py`, `tests/test_fourth_review_stopped_words_v1535.py`.

- **A launch recipe never sets an account home and never writes in the terminal's folder; the terminal says so, and takes its own files with it on Close** (v1.539.0, fourth review TM1, MS4a, TM3, TM4, TM5, C-TM-1, C-TM-2, C-TM-3; owner decisions D8, D9 option B).
  The terminal's account decides `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`, `CODEX_HOME` and `GROK_HOME`; `TerminalManager._prepare_recipe` drops one a preparer answers, in either shape, and logs it. Claude's file, Pi's extension and a Pi-without-`-e`'s adapter are the terminal's own, `<home>/terminals-mcp/<id>.json|.js|.mjs`, passed by absolute path (`--mcp-config … --strict-mcp-config` with server `iron-jarvis-terminal`, never the connected apps' `iron-jarvis`; Pi's `-e`). Codex gets bare `-c mcp_servers.iron-jarvis-code.url=… -c mcp_servers.iron-jarvis-code.bearer_token_env_var=IRONJARVIS_MCP_TOKEN` only when its help names `--config`. Both Close routes call `recipes.remove_terminal_files`; start-up sweeps files of terminals that did not come back (`sweep_terminal_files`, `older_than` the boot). `recipes.leftovers_in` names an older version's `.jarvis-codex` or old-shaped `.mcp.json` entry, never touched. The row's `tools {note, leftovers, limitations}` is the ONE place these sentences show (`routes/terminals._tools_note`, `TerminalPane.paneToolsLines`); the note is withheld if a `config_writes` path lies in the folder. Launch-with-tools takes `account` (the dialog names it: `toolsAccountOf`, never an account it will not send). `POST /terminals/not-reopened/{id}/retry {"shell": "default"}` reopens on the usual shell (`usual_shell` on a program-gone row). The socket's restart-at-size runs as one task per socket (`session.restart_possible` gates it), and `RESTART_FAILED_NOTE` is said once per failure streak. A timed-out skill read is 503 `SKILL_LIBRARY_SLOW`; a missing skill stays 404. `PosixPtyBackend.start` reports a failed exec through a close-on-exec pipe and raises like Windows (exit 127 stays "ended by itself"); `POST /terminals` words an `OSError`. Pins: `tests/test_fourth_review_terminal_recipes_v1539.py`, `tests/test_fourth_review_terminal_routes_v1539.py`, `tests/test_fourth_review_pty_v1539.py` (its real-fork tests run only on Linux), `dashboard/__tests__/fourth-review-terminals-v1539.test.tsx`.

- **A timed-out open of the agent's browser writes ONE warning saying how far the page got, and its process facts never hold the open more than `SLOW_OPEN_FACTS_S` past its own limit** (v1.548.2, Build › Code follow-up, item 1).
  `EdgeBackend.goto` traces each open (`_OpenTrace`: rules asked, let go, answered, arrived, HTML read, loaded) through the route, response, `framenavigated`, `domcontentloaded` and `load` handlers; only an open that ran out of time logs, and the facts (Edge's processes, the driver's priority, the processors' load) are read off the loop for at most 2 s past the open's limit, counted from when `goto` began, so the hard bound (`OPEN_TIMEOUT_S + HARD_BOUND_EXTRA_S`) keeps 8 of its 10 s. A real-Edge test is named `test_real_edge_*`: the CI hooks in `tests/_real_edge_report.py` key on that name to print what the machine was doing when one fails, also when it passes on its retry. The incident and the CI side: `ci.md`. Pins: `tests/test_build_browser_slow_open.py`.

- **Before and after pictures under a coding reply are Iron Jarvis's OWN, taken only when asked (the message's words, Show me, or the setting), the before started as the reply begins and kept by the first screen write BEFORE it lands, and they never reach a model** (v1.553.0; R2.15, phase 27-a of `docs/next-leaps/07-coding-github-evidence-scoreboard-sealed-room.md`; decisions D-27.1 changed and D-27.2). `harness/evidence`: `takes(setting, text, address, by_person)` arms a turn in `routes/build.start_build_turn` beside the Browser's address (a person's turn with a page open, and `asks(text)` ("show me it works") or `Config.reply_pictures is True`; None = never chosen = off, offered ONCE by the first Show me through `offer_due`/`mark_offered`, answered with `PUT /settings/values`). `by_person` is `started_by == BY_PERSON`: a carry-on a person PRESSED (Allow on the plan, Continue, Continue with another account) is theirs, its words read from their newest question (`_person_question`, never the hidden carry-on text); only the night, CI, Resume when ready and a restart are Iron Jarvis's own (`STARTED_WORDS` for CI alone, whose words are Iron Jarvis's; a reply it carried on, Resume when ready or a restart, says `CARRIED_WORDS`, and a night task, the person's words queued for tonight, says `NIGHT_WORDS`: never "not a message of yours"). The before picture STARTS as the reply begins (`start_before`, right after `logs.start`, under the turn's Local only scope read with `effective_local_only` because the lane sets its own later; not in plan mode; the turn task's end calls `stop_before`): a real Edge one-shot measured 6-20 s on this PC, more than the hold. `registry.invoke` calls `before_write` right before the undo capture for a REVERSIBLE call: the first screen-file write (`SCREEN_SUFFIXES`, paths from the writer's own arguments, `apply_patch`'s file lines) waits on the turn's lock for that picture, at most `BEFORE_WAIT_S` (8 s), and keeps it; still not there, it is cancelled (never kept: it could show the change), the write goes ahead and the record says why; one that failed before the write is taken again then; parallel writes and helpers wait on the same lock; a reply no screen write took it from keeps nothing. BOTH lanes call `at_end(made_docs)` LAST on the done frame / POST answer (MIRROR NOTE): None when no screen file REALLY changed (each screen file's state before its first write is kept, read through `safe_path`, so a failed write is no change), the Show me mark `{kind, screen, items: [], note, beforeNote}` when the turn takes nothing (`beforeNote` = `no_before_words`: started by Iron Jarvis / not asked / no page open — the true reason), else the after picture (the stream lane takes it BEFORE reading the owed notes). `routes/build_evidence`: `POST /build/sessions/{id}/evidence` (Show me: after now, refused in words while a reply runs, archived, no page, a public address; and — it pictures the page AS IT IS NOW — refused for any reply but the session's NEWEST, for one that changed no screen file, and for one that already has its after (never replaced): `not_offered`, asked again after the picture and before it is kept, `amend_reply(expect=...)`; a Local only session's page opens behind the refusing proxy; `amend_reply` writes the line) and `GET .../evidence/{turn}/{item}` (the picture, ids checked, 404 "no longer kept"). Kept in `<home>/reply-evidence/<session>/<turn>/` with `evidence.json` (where = host and path only, never the query; each picture's `at` is when the browser TOOK it, `ActResult.taken_at` passed to `_save(at=)`, never the save's: an early before is saved only at the first screen write; sha256), newest `KEEP_REPLIES` (40) replies per session, moved to the trash with the session (`trash_session_evidence` beside `trash_session_shots`). The pictures are drawn by `components/harness/ReplyPictures` under a settled reply (one column below 640 px), opened in the chat's lightbox with no Open link (the address carries the token); Show me is drawn only under the newest reply with no after (`showMeOffered`), and "show me it works" typed ALONE presses that reply's own Show me (`showMeReply`, `requestShowMe`). Show me reads the conversation's JSON only in a worker (`asyncio.to_thread`, pinned by `test_show_me_reads_the_conversation_off_the_event_loop`). KNOWN: a screen file changed only by a command is not noticed; the after at the reply's end holds the done frame up to `AFTER_WAIT_S` with no word on screen; a before kept by the first screen write of a reply then stopped or failed stays until the prune; `amend_reply(expect=)` re-checks only that no after has arrived, so a reply that starts in the milliseconds between `still()` and the write could still be pictured; the one-shot path was not driven on the packaged build. Pins: `tests/test_reply_evidence.py`, `tests/test_reply_evidence_keep.py`, `dashboard/__tests__/reply-evidence.test.tsx`.

- **Build › Code's `/` list is asked for each time the menu OPENS, the start box has one, and an answer says when the list is still being read** (v1.557.0, the owner: "the / commands for skills dont work. These should be available at any point within this prompting window").
  Read from the live app first: the start box had no menu, the tools listing gave Claude Code 0 commands, and a session's first look answered 0 while `hosted/claude_catalog` read the folder (90 a moment later). DAEMON: `HostedService.commands_state` answers `{commands, reading}` (`reading` = `claude_catalog.reading(folder)`, Claude Code only, never while the session's own process has listed); the session view carries `commands_reading`, `GET /build/app/sessions/{id}/commands` carries `reading`, and `GET /build/app/commands?harness=&folder=` (`start_commands`, sync, on the threadpool) is the list for a session that does not exist yet. With NO folder Claude Code's list is read in `claude_catalog.no_folder_dir(home)` (`<home>/hosted/claude-probe/no-folder`, made on the reading thread): its commands plus the user's own commands and skills (measured on claude 2.1.323: 67 rows in 2.9 s, 20 of them not built in). A folder `fs_policy.root_problem` refuses is never read (the no-folder list is answered). The tools listing still starts no read. DASHBOARD: `components/buildCode/useToolCommands` reads on the menu's rising edge and looks again every `COMMANDS_LOOK_AGAIN_MS` while `reading`, at most `COMMANDS_LOOKS` times; the newest look wins over the view's list, then the tools listing's (an older daemon, whose read fails, still shows those). `StartComposer` has the menu (`code-start-slash-menu`, rows `code-start-command-<name>`, `code-start-slash-status`, `code-start-slash-note`); both boxes draw a row with `CommandRowBody` and say `slashStatusWords` instead of showing nothing. After a pick the caret is set in the layout effect as well: the key press that picked also reports the selection from before the text changed, which kept the menu open. KNOWN: the menu opens only on `/` at the start of a line (unchanged); the start box has no `@` list; not looked at on the packaged build (memory was short on the day), so the menu's place above the centred start box is unseen. Pins: `tests/test_build_app_start_commands_v1557.py`, `dashboard/__tests__/build-code-start-slash-v1557.test.tsx`.
